<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession. The departure marks a leadership change at Nvidia.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。这意味着英伟达将发生领导层变动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cs.cmu.edu/learn/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon is putting computer science courses on YouTube for free</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学将计算机科学课程免费放到 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">cs.cmu.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon’s Kenneth C. Griffin School of Computer Science is publishing free lectures on YouTube.</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学肯尼斯·C·格里芬计算机科学学院正在 YouTube 上发布免费课程讲座。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon’s Kenneth C. Griffin School of Computer Science is publishing free lectures on YouTube. Self-paced courseware is planned as the next step. The release gives learners access to the school’s computer science material without charge.</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学肯尼斯·C·格里芬计算机科学学院正在 YouTube 上发布免费课程讲座。下一步计划推出可自主学习的课程材料。这将让学习者免费接触该学院的计算机科学内容。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/11/17/upshot/school-phone-bans-study-scores.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://static01.nyt.com/images/2026/11/17/multimedia/17up-phone-pouch1-jlkf/17up-phone-pouch1-jlkf-facebookJumbo.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nytimes.com' data-c='#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/11/17/upshot/school-phone-bans-study-scores.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New Evidence Shows School Phone Bans Are Gradually Improving Test Scores</span><span class='zh'>新证据显示，学校禁用手机正逐步提高考试成绩</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements. Schools with strict policies requiring students to lock phones in pouches for the entire school day saw math and reading gains equivalent to roughly one or two additional weeks of schooling per year. The study was published by Harvard’s Center for Education Policy Research and is the first national study to track individual students’ growth in this way.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。要求学生在整个上课日将手机锁入袋中的学校，数学和阅读成绩提升幅度相当于每年额外增加约一至两周的学习。该研究由哈佛教育政策研究中心发布，是首项以这种方式追踪个体学生成长的全国性研究。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1286</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50530163' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>642</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50538240" data-id="50538240" data-tags="Security" data-score="1176" data-comments="329" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50538240" data-time="1794949888">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://calif.io/java4shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Java4Shell: RCE in OpenJDK when processing Base64</span><span class='zh'>Java4Shell：处理 Base64 时 OpenJDK 中的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">calif.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder. OpenJDK is described as the most popular Java runtime. The vulnerability has a CVSS 4.0 score of 9.2 out of 10, rated Critical. Its severity could make affected OpenJDK deployments a high-priority patching target.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。OpenJDK 被称为最流行的 Java 运行时。该漏洞在 CVSS 4.0 中获得 9.2 分（满分 10 分），评级为“严重”。这一评级意味着受影响的 OpenJDK 部署可能需要优先修补。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://status.cloud.microsoft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Service degradation on Microsoft 365</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 服务降级</span></a><span class="dom">status.cloud.microsoft</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 is experiencing service degradation.</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 正在经历服务降级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 is experiencing service degradation. The available material does not specify which services are affected, the cause, or the expected resolution time. Users may therefore encounter disruptions while the incident is being addressed.</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 正在经历服务降级。现有材料没有说明受影响的具体服务、原因或预计恢复时间。因此，用户在问题处理期间可能会遇到服务中断。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/blog/ai-code-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/img/en/ai-code-open-source.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jakub.jankiewicz.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/blog/ai-code-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All AI-generated code should be Open Source</span><span class='zh'>所有 AI 生成的代码都应开源</span></a><span class="dom">jakub.jankiewicz.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that all code generated with AI assistance should be released as open source or under a CC0 license.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张，所有借助 AI 生成的代码都应以开源许可证或 CC0 许可发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that all code generated with AI assistance should be released as open source or under a CC0 license. The argument is based on AI models being trained on large amounts of publicly available open-source code, as well as Stack Overflow and related sites whose Creative Commons licenses generally require attribution. The author says turning that shared knowledge into proprietary code resembles license laundering.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张，所有借助 AI 生成的代码都应以开源许可证或 CC0 许可发布。其理由是，AI 模型训练使用了大量公开的开源代码，以及通常要求署名的 Stack Overflow 和相关网站内容。作者认为，将这些共享知识转化为专有闭源代码近似于“许可证洗白”。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>10.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1094</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50507303' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>595</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50493251" data-id="50493251" data-tags="AI|Business|Hardware" data-score="1036" data-comments="621" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493251" data-time="1794624779">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.techpowerup.com/354814/nvidia-allegedly-engineered-scarcity-by-funding-ai-startups-to-buy-its-gpus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.techpowerup.com/img/ooCln2q3jkKNGHen.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='techpowerup.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techpowerup.com/354814/nvidia-allegedly-engineered-scarcity-by-funding-ai-startups-to-buy-its-gpus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia allegedly engineered scarcity by funding AI startups to buy its GPUs</span><span class='zh'>英伟达被指通过资助 AI 初创公司购买 GPU，人为制造供应紧张</span></a><span class="dom">techpowerup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new report, citing The Wall Street Journal, alleges that NVIDIA increased the GPU and memory shortage by investing in AI startups that then bought NVIDIA GPUs and built data centers.</span><span class='zh'>一份援引《华尔街日报》的新报告称，英伟达通过投资 AI 初创公司，促使这些公司购买英伟达 GPU 并建设数据中心，从而加剧了 GPU 和内存短缺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new report, citing The Wall Street Journal, alleges that NVIDIA increased the GPU and memory shortage by investing in AI startups that then bought NVIDIA GPUs and built data centers. The allegation emerged after Jensen Huang’s departure as NVIDIA’s CEO, according to the supplied report. The claim concerns whether the company’s financing activity helped inflate demand for its own hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一份援引《华尔街日报》的新报告称，英伟达通过投资 AI 初创公司，促使这些公司购买英伟达 GPU 并建设数据中心，从而加剧了 GPU 和内存短缺。报告称，这一指控是在 Jensen Huang 离任英伟达 CEO 后出现的。争议焦点在于，英伟达的融资活动是否帮助推高了对其自有硬件的需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1036</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493251' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>621</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50552424" data-id="50552424" data-tags="AI|Science" data-score="1016" data-comments="761" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50552424" data-time="1795037647">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-neon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-4-neon__blog-header__sm.width-1300.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.google' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-neon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Neon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Neon</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Neon, a model aimed at complex coding, knowledge work, and scientific workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Neon，面向复杂编程、知识工作和科学工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Neon, a model aimed at complex coding, knowledge work, and scientific workflows. The company says it offers frontier-level intelligence with fast end-to-end task completion at a lower cost. The model is presented by Google DeepMind’s senior vice president and chief AI architect.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Neon，面向复杂编程、知识工作和科学工作流。Google 表示，该模型以更低成本提供前沿级智能，并能快速完成端到端任务。该模型由 Google DeepMind 高级副总裁兼首席 AI 架构师发布。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1016</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50552424' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>761</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50465829" data-id="50465829" data-tags="Business|Finance|AI|Science" data-score="996" data-comments="904" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465829" data-time="1794484001">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ipo.anthropic.com/assets/share-image-eAr8nJRP.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ipo.anthropic.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic IPO</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 筹备首次公开募股</span></a><span class="dom">ipo.anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information. The company is organized as a public benefit corporation focused on securing AI’s benefits and mitigating its risks. It says AI could accelerate scientific innovation, increase economic growth, help cure major diseases, and address long-standing problems if managed well.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。该公司采用公益公司架构，致力于发挥人工智能的益处并降低其风险。Anthropic 表示，如果得到妥善管理，人工智能可能加速科学创新、提高经济增长率、推动重大疾病治疗，并解决长期未能解决的问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>996</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465829' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>904</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50499017" data-id="50499017" data-tags="Privacy|Startups|AI" data-score="974" data-comments="372" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50499017" data-time="1794678190">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.crissyfield.de/research/lovable-en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.crissyfield.de/images/research-lovable-opengraph.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='crissyfield.de' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.crissyfield.de/research/lovable-en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lovable apps expose passport details and more than 200K private messages</span><span class='zh'>Lovable 应用暴露护照信息和超过 20 万条私人消息</span></a><span class="dom">crissyfield.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 159,041 Lovable projects found many cases in which personal and confidential information was accessible without logging in, including passport details and more than 200,000 private messages.</span><span class='zh'>对 159,041 个 Lovable 项目的分析发现，许多项目无需登录即可访问个人和机密信息，其中包括护照详情以及超过 20 万条私人消息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 159,041 Lovable projects found many cases in which personal and confidential information was accessible without logging in, including passport details and more than 200,000 private messages. Lovable is a Stockholm-based Swedish AI startup founded in 2023 by Anton Osika and Fabian Hedin, whose platform generates web applications from plain-language descriptions. The findings show that building applications with AI does not remove the need for careful handling of sensitive data.</span><span class='zh'>对 159,041 个 Lovable 项目的分析发现，许多项目无需登录即可访问个人和机密信息，其中包括护照详情以及超过 20 万条私人消息。Lovable 是一家总部位于斯德哥尔摩、由 Anton Osika 和 Fabian Hedin 于 2023 年创立的瑞典 AI 初创公司，其平台可根据自然语言描述生成 Web 应用。这些发现表明，使用 AI 构建应用并不能免除对敏感数据进行谨慎处理的要求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>974</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50499017' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>372</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50517488" data-id="50517488" data-tags="Business" data-score="954" data-comments="551" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50517488" data-time="1794836347">
<a class="thumb" href="http://groundworkcollaborative.org/news/amazons-decoy-deals-groundwork-investigation-reveals-nearly-1-in-3-black-friday-and-cyber-monday-deals-advertised-by-amazon-offered-no-savings-to-shoppers-nearly-1-in-10-w/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='http://groundworkcollaborative.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/GWC_CL_Cover_110526-1-791x1024.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='groundworkcollaborative.org' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="http://groundworkcollaborative.org/news/amazons-decoy-deals-groundwork-investigation-reveals-nearly-1-in-3-black-friday-and-cyber-monday-deals-advertised-by-amazon-offered-no-savings-to-shoppers-nearly-1-in-10-w/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Many of Amazon’s Black Friday and Cyber Monday “deals” offered no savings</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊许多黑色星期五和网络星期一“优惠”并没有省钱</span></a><span class="dom">groundworkcollaborative.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings. The deals were presented as discounts even though shoppers often paid no less than usual.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。这些商品虽然以折扣形式展示，消费者实际支付的价格却往往并未降低。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.rust-lang.org/2026/11/12/Rust-1.100.0/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust 1.100</span><span class='zh'>Rust 1.100</span></a><span class="dom">blog.rust-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0. Rust is described as a language for building reliable and efficient software. Users with an existing rustup installation can install the new version through rustup.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。Rust 被定位为用于构建可靠、高效软件的编程语言。已经通过 rustup 安装 Rust 的用户可以使用 rustup 获取新版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/quad9-withdrawal-from-france/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quad9&#x27;s withdrawal from France: A canary in the coal mine</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 退出法国：煤矿里的金丝雀</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Swiss nonprofit Quad9 said it will withdraw its free public DNS resolver from France after months of litigation.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士非营利组织 Quad9 表示，将在经历数月诉讼后退出法国免费公共 DNS 解析服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Swiss nonprofit Quad9 said it will withdraw its free public DNS resolver from France after months of litigation. A French court imposed daily financial penalties because Quad9 did not block domains linked to alleged copyright infringement. Quad9 said the choice was between crippling penalties and changing its service in ways that would undermine user privacy and security. The case is presented as a warning about the consequences of mandating DNS-level blocking.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士非营利组织 Quad9 表示，将在经历数月诉讼后退出法国免费公共 DNS 解析服务。法国法院因其未屏蔽涉嫌侵犯版权的域名，对 Quad9 处以每日罚款。Quad9 表示，它只能在承受可能令组织陷入困境的处罚，或改变服务并损害用户隐私与安全之间作出选择。该事件被视为强制实施 DNS 层拦截可能带来后果的警示。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>883</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50538994' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>432</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50513215" data-id="50513215" data-tags="AI" data-score="854" data-comments="471" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50513215" data-time="1794795284">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen4-max" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen4-Max</span><span class='zh'>Qwen4-Max</span></a><span class="dom">qwen.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen4-Max is described as supporting chat, image and video understanding, image generation, document processing, web search integration, tool use, and artifacts.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen4-Max 被描述为支持聊天、图像和视频理解、图像生成、文档处理、联网搜索、工具调用及 artifacts。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen4-Max is described as supporting chat, image and video understanding, image generation, document processing, web search integration, tool use, and artifacts. It is positioned as a model or service with functionality spanning multiple input and output types.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen4-Max 被描述为支持聊天、图像和视频理解、图像生成、文档处理、联网搜索、工具调用及 artifacts。它定位为覆盖多种输入和输出形式的模型或服务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/nvidia-employees-petition-for-jensen-huangs-return/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia employees petition to bring back Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>英伟达员工请愿要求让黄仁勋回归</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang. The post was submitted before Huang’s resignation by a longtime engineer and asked the board to consider employee views before taking action. Some employees are now asking the board to find a way to bring Huang back despite reports of multiple extramarital affairs and his abrupt resignation.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。该帖子由一名长期任职的工程师在黄仁勋辞职前提交，要求董事会在采取行动前考虑员工意见。尽管有报道称黄仁勋存在多段婚外情并突然辞职，仍有部分员工要求董事会设法让他回归。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottjg.com/posts/2026-11-11-reverse-engineering-my-coway-air-purifier-to-ditch-the-cloud/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering my Coway air purifier to ditch the cloud</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程我的 Coway 空气净化器，摆脱云端控制</span></a><span class="dom">scottjg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed. The author describes buying a Coway Airmega to avoid breathing wildfire soot and reverse-engineering it to remove its dependence on cloud services. The project reflects a broader effort to keep household devices locally controlled.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。作者介绍了自己购买 Coway Airmega，并对其进行逆向工程，以摆脱对云端服务的依赖。这一项目体现了将家用设备控制权保留在本地的尝试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>785</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474959' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>222</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50554045" data-id="50554045" data-tags="Culture" data-score="782" data-comments="391" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554045" data-time="1795046029">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://violetpixel.com/blog/2026-11-17-we-need-smart-people-who-give-a-shit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We need smart people who give a shit</span><span class='zh'>我们需要那些聪明且真正上心的人</span></a><span class="dom">violetpixel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that complex systems are held together by smart people who care deeply about their work.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，复杂系统依靠聪明且真正关心工作的人维系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that complex systems are held together by smart people who care deeply about their work. This view becomes especially apparent when examining how difficult systems function, or how close they are to failing. The essay begins from that observation about the people who keep complicated systems operating.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，复杂系统依靠聪明且真正关心工作的人维系。这一点在观察复杂系统如何运转，或如何勉强维持运转时尤其明显。文章以这一关于维持复杂系统运行者的观察为出发点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>782</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554045' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>391</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50547620" data-id="50547620" data-tags="Finance|Security|Business" data-score="775" data-comments="258" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547620" data-time="1795016313">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inside-db.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inside Deutsche Bank</span><span class='zh'>德意志银行</span></a><span class="dom">inside-db.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A major investigation into Deutsche Bank is based on a leak of more than 15 million files.</span><span class='zh'>一项针对德意志银行的重大调查建立在超过 1500 万份泄露文件的基础上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A major investigation into Deutsche Bank is based on a leak of more than 15 million files. The files cover sensitive functions including anti-financial-crime work, sanctions monitoring, internal auditing, and decisions about whether risky clients should remain customers. The investigation portrays flaws in the bank&#x27;s culture, systemic blind spots, and tensions between profit-seeking and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>一项针对德意志银行的重大调查建立在超过 1500 万份泄露文件的基础上。这些文件涉及反金融犯罪、制裁监控、内部审计，以及决定高风险客户是否继续留存等敏感职能。调查呈现出该行文化中的缺陷、系统性盲点，以及追求利润与合规要求之间的矛盾。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>775</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547620' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>258</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50542705" data-id="50542705" data-tags="AI" data-score="759" data-comments="684" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50542705" data-time="1794984446">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nicolasbouliane.com/blog/death-by-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nicolasbouliane.com/images/content2x/all-about-berlin-traffic-loss.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nicolasbouliane.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nicolasbouliane.com/blog/death-by-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is killing All About Berlin</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在扼杀 All About Berlin</span></a><span class="dom">nicolasbouliane.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The operator of All About Berlin says the site&#x27;s traffic fell by 70% to 75% after years of growth, attributing the decline to Google&#x27;s AI Overviews.</span><span class='zh'>All About Berlin 的运营者表示，该网站在多年增长后，流量因 Google AI Overviews 下跌了 70% 至 75%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The operator of All About Berlin says the site&#x27;s traffic fell by 70% to 75% after years of growth, attributing the decline to Google&#x27;s AI Overviews. Search pages now place an AI-generated answer trained on the site&#x27;s content before the link to the site itself. The change has had a devastating effect on the site&#x27;s traffic.</span><span class='zh'>All About Berlin 的运营者表示，该网站在多年增长后，流量因 Google AI Overviews 下跌了 70% 至 75%。如今搜索页面会先展示基于该网站内容训练的 AI 生成答案，最后才显示指向网站的链接。这一变化对网站流量造成了严重打击。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>759</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50542705' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>684</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50519231" data-id="50519231" data-tags="Security" data-score="756" data-comments="316" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50519231" data-time="1794845616">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.harrison.page/garage-door-spam/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Garage Door Spam</span><span class='zh'>车库门垃圾通知</span></a><span class="dom">blog.harrison.page</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A garage door opener initially sent notifications whenever the door opened or closed, which made unexpected openings seem meaningful when the owner was away.</span><span class='zh'>一款车库门开启器最初会在车门开关时发送通知，因此人在外出时，异常开门会显得格外重要。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A garage door opener initially sent notifications whenever the door opened or closed, which made unexpected openings seem meaningful when the owner was away. The owner continued using the myQ app despite its advertisements because the notifications appeared trustworthy.</span><span class='zh'>一款车库门开启器最初会在车门开关时发送通知，因此人在外出时，异常开门会显得格外重要。尽管 myQ 应用广告很多，用户仍因信任这些通知而继续使用它。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xusheng.dev/posts/windows-coding-agent-telemetry/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Windows 11 is collecting data on which coding agents and developer tools you use</span><span class='zh'>Windows 11 正收集你使用哪些编程代理和开发工具的数据</span></a><span class="dom">xusheng.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering of Windows 11’s MissionControlAggregator found coding-agent and developer-tool usage bits in a diagnostic ETW report.</span><span class='zh'>对 Windows 11 的 MissionControlAggregator 进行逆向分析后，研究者在诊断 ETW 报告中发现了编程代理和开发工具的使用标记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering of Windows 11’s MissionControlAggregator found coding-agent and developer-tool usage bits in a diagnostic ETW report. The observed list includes Codex, Claude, Copilot, Cursor, Python, .NET, GCC, and Visual Studio Code executables. The finding raises questions about why a Windows system DLL records the use of these tools.</span><span class='zh'>对 Windows 11 的 MissionControlAggregator 进行逆向分析后，研究者在诊断 ETW 报告中发现了编程代理和开发工具的使用标记。相关列表包括 Codex、Claude、Copilot、Cursor，以及 Python、.NET、GCC 和 Visual Studio Code 等可执行文件。这一发现引出了一个问题：Windows 系统 DLL 为何要记录这些工具的使用情况。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>711</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524736' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>592</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50540178" data-id="50540178" data-tags="AI" data-score="690" data-comments="539" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50540178" data-time="1794961078">
<a class="thumb" href="https://revscope.co/blog/search-seo-geo-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=revscope.co&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>revscope.co</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://revscope.co/blog/search-seo-geo-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s AI answers reducing search traffic by 40%</span><span class='zh'>谷歌 AI 答案让搜索流量下降 40%</span></a><span class="dom">revscope.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A randomized experiment found that Google AI Overviews reduce clicks to external websites by about 40% among US desktop users.</span><span class='zh'>一项随机实验发现，在美国桌面用户中，谷歌 AI Overviews 会让外部网站获得的点击量减少约 40%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A randomized experiment found that Google AI Overviews reduce clicks to external websites by about 40% among US desktop users. The summaries now appear on more than half of the US keywords tracked by DataForSEO, up from 6% two years earlier. Generative engine optimization has not yet demonstrated a way to offset the lost traffic, while AI assistants sent websites only 0.3% of their traffic in March. The findings indicate that traditional search rankings bring substantially fewer visits when an AI answer appears above them.</span><span class='zh'>一项随机实验发现，在美国桌面用户中，谷歌 AI Overviews 会让外部网站获得的点击量减少约 40%。根据 DataForSEO 的跟踪数据，这些摘要如今出现在超过一半的美国关键词结果中，而两年前这一比例仅为 6%。生成式引擎优化尚未证明能弥补这部分流量损失；3 月份，AI 助手为网站带来的流量仅占 0.3%。这表明，当 AI 答案出现在搜索结果上方时，传统排名带来的访问量会显著下降。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>690</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50540178' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>539</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50524767" data-id="50524767" data-tags="Security|Programming|Finance" data-score="669" data-comments="264" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524767" data-time="1794870034">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.report-uri.com/worlds-largest-brands-infected-with-malware-via-optimizely/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Optimizely compromised, served malware on Zoom, GitLab</span><span class='zh'>Optimizely 遭入侵，Zoom、GitLab 等网站被投放恶意软件</span></a><span class="dom">blog.report-uri.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>On November 16, 2026, malicious JavaScript was served through Optimizely’s Web experimentation platform to visitors of 178 organizations, including Zoom, the BBC, CNN, Salesforce, GitLab, IKEA, HSBC, and others.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 16 日，Optimizely 的 Web 实验平台向 178 家机构的网站访客投放了恶意 JavaScript，受影响机构包括 Zoom、BBC、CNN、Salesforce、GitLab、IKEA 和 HSBC 等。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>On November 16, 2026, malicious JavaScript was served through Optimizely’s Web experimentation platform to visitors of 178 organizations, including Zoom, the BBC, CNN, Salesforce, GitLab, IKEA, HSBC, and others. The malware could replace cryptocurrency wallet addresses, skim payment-card data, steal cookies and screenshots, and deliver the Coruna and DarkSword iOS exploit kits on iPhones and iPads. Cryptocurrency had already been stolen in transactions verified as belonging to the attackers.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 16 日，Optimizely 的 Web 实验平台向 178 家机构的网站访客投放了恶意 JavaScript，受影响机构包括 Zoom、BBC、CNN、Salesforce、GitLab、IKEA 和 HSBC 等。该恶意软件能够替换加密货币钱包地址、窃取支付卡数据、Cookie 和屏幕截图，并在 iPhone 和 iPad 上投递 Coruna 与 DarkSword iOS 漏洞利用套件。已有加密货币在被确认属于攻击者的交易中遭窃取。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnn.com/2026/11/17/politics/highly-classified-national-security-communications-network-goes-down" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Highly classified US national security communications network goes down</span><span class='zh'>高度机密的美国国家安全通信网络发生故障</span></a><span class="dom">cnn.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The highly classified network used by the National Security Agency for internal communications is down, according to six people familiar with the matter.</span><span class='zh'>据六名知情人士透露，美国国家安全局用于内部通信的高度机密网络目前发生故障。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The highly classified network used by the National Security Agency for internal communications is down, according to six people familiar with the matter. The reported outage affects a communications system supporting the agency’s internal operations.</span><span class='zh'>据六名知情人士透露，美国国家安全局用于内部通信的高度机密网络目前发生故障。此次中断影响了支持该机构内部运作的通信系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>664</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532849' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>748</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50558533" data-id="50558533" data-tags="Open Source" data-score="663" data-comments="323" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558533" data-time="1795085909">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://element.io/blog/how-the-european-commission-rolled-out-matrix-to-more-than-40-000-staff/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How the European Commission rolled out Matrix to more than 40k staff</span><span class='zh'>欧洲委员会如何将 Matrix 推广给超过 4 万名员工</span></a><span class="dom">element.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The European Commission moved from a Matrix proof of concept in December 2025 to a sovereign communication tool deployed on every Commission laptop by September 2026.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲委员会从 2025 年 12 月的 Matrix 概念验证起步，到 2026 年 9 月将一套主权通信工具部署到每台委员会笔记本电脑上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The European Commission moved from a Matrix proof of concept in December 2025 to a sovereign communication tool deployed on every Commission laptop by September 2026. Built on Matrix by Element and operated by the Commission itself, the service now supports more than 40,000 users. Commission officials described the operational challenges of scaling the system and how they addressed them.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲委员会从 2025 年 12 月的 Matrix 概念验证起步，到 2026 年 9 月将一套主权通信工具部署到每台委员会笔记本电脑上。该工具由 Element 基于 Matrix 构建，并由委员会自行运营，目前已服务超过 4 万名用户。委员会官员还介绍了系统扩展过程中遇到的运营难题及应对方式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>663</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558533' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>323</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50534631" data-id="50534631" data-tags="Business|Finance" data-score="657" data-comments="295" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50534631" data-time="1794935526">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ft.com/content/dbde7a30-548c-4f9d-a4be-7898a0c24dcf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia stopped disclosing Singapore sales as revenues hit $104B</span><span class='zh'>英伟达营收达到 1040 亿美元后停止披露新加坡销售额</span></a><span class="dom">ft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia stopped disclosing its Singapore sales as its revenue reached $104 billion.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达营收达到 1040 亿美元后，停止披露其在新加坡的销售额。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia stopped disclosing its Singapore sales as its revenue reached $104 billion. No further details about the reporting change or its reasons are established here.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达营收达到 1040 亿美元后，停止披露其在新加坡的销售额。现有信息未说明这一报告变化的具体原因或其他细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www1.wdr.de/nrw/rheinland/rhein-erft-kreis/technischer-defekt-chlor-phantasialand-100.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 outage leads to chlorine gas poisoning of 15 hotel guests</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 故障导致 15 名酒店客人氯气中毒</span></a><span class="dom">www1.wdr.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool. Fifteen guests were injured as a result.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。结果造成 15 名客人受伤。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://so.long.thanks.fish/why-pi-when-i-have-an-old-phone/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://so.long.thanks.fish/why-pi-when-i-have-an-old-phone/redmi-leds-1200.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='so.long.thanks.fish' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">39</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://so.long.thanks.fish/why-pi-when-i-have-an-old-phone/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why get an expensive Pi when I have an old phone?</span><span class='zh'>既然有旧手机，为什么还要买昂贵的树莓派？</span></a><span class="dom">so.long.thanks.fish</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project uses a rooted second-hand Redmi smartphone as a camera and light controller instead of building the system around a Raspberry Pi.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目改用一部已取得 root 权限的二手 Redmi 手机来充当摄像头和灯光控制器，而不是围绕树莓派搭建系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project uses a rooted second-hand Redmi smartphone as a camera and light controller instead of building the system around a Raspberry Pi. The phone already provides a high-resolution camera, GPS, storage, battery, and mobile data, while the original Raspberry Pi design required adding an Arducam 64MP camera and a GPS module. The approach could reduce the number of components and the project’s growing bill.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目改用一部已取得 root 权限的二手 Redmi 手机来充当摄像头和灯光控制器，而不是围绕树莓派搭建系统。手机已经具备高分辨率摄像头、GPS、存储、电池和移动数据功能；原先的树莓派方案则需要额外加入 Arducam 6400 万像素摄像头和 GPS 模块。这种做法有望减少组件数量，并控制不断上涨的项目成本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>617</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50440709' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>322</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50545958" data-id="50545958" data-tags="Legal" data-score="603" data-comments="223" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50545958" data-time="1795007882">
<a class="thumb" href="https://techstackups.com/articles/quad9-leaves-france-dns-blocking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://techstackups.com/img/articles/quad9-leaves-france/cover.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='techstackups.com' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">40</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techstackups.com/articles/quad9-leaves-france-dns-blocking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DNS resolvers are the wrong place to fight piracy</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 将退出法国：DNS 解析器不该成为打击盗版的场所</span></a><span class="dom">techstackups.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The nonprofit DNS resolver Quad9 will stop answering queries from France on November 24, 2026, rather than comply with a Paris court order sought by beIN Sports.</span><span class='zh'>非营利 DNS 解析器 Quad9 将于 2026 年 11 月 24 日停止响应来自法国的查询，以拒绝执行 beIN Sports 申请的巴黎法院命令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The nonprofit DNS resolver Quad9 will stop answering queries from France on November 24, 2026, rather than comply with a Paris court order sought by beIN Sports. The court fined Quad9 for refusing to block pirate sports-streaming domains, and roughly 20 similar cases are still pending. The dispute raises concerns that forcing public DNS resolvers to enforce domain blocking could undermine services beyond its intended target.</span><span class='zh'>非营利 DNS 解析器 Quad9 将于 2026 年 11 月 24 日停止响应来自法国的查询，以拒绝执行 beIN Sports 申请的巴黎法院命令。Quad9 因拒绝屏蔽盗版体育流媒体域名而被罚款，法国目前还有约 20 起类似案件待审。这场争议引发了对强制公共 DNS 解析器执行域名屏蔽可能造成更广泛影响的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iter.ca/post/ubo-uxss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Universal XSS in uBlock Origin from website-driven element picker</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的网站驱动元素选择器存在通用 XSS</span></a><span class="dom">iter.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A one-click universal XSS vulnerability affected the Firefox version of uBlock Origin through its element picker.</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的 Firefox 版本元素选择器存在一个可通过一次点击触发的通用 XSS 漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A one-click universal XSS vulnerability affected the Firefox version of uBlock Origin through its element picker. A malicious website could trick the extension into adding arbitrary trusted filters, which can inject JavaScript into other websites and execute it in their contexts. The issue has been fixed; a related but less severe problem also affected the MV3-based uBO Lite.</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的 Firefox 版本元素选择器存在一个可通过一次点击触发的通用 XSS 漏洞。恶意网站可以诱使扩展添加任意受信过滤规则，而受信规则能够向其他网站注入 JavaScript，并在这些网站的上下文中执行。该问题现已修复；基于 MV3 的 uBO Lite 也受到一个相关但危害较小的问题影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KDE: The Documentary tells the story of the open-source community that emerged in 1996 to offer computer users a free alternative to proprietary desktops such as Windows.</span><span class='zh'>《KDE：纪录片》讲述了这一开源社区的故事：它于 1996 年兴起，为计算机用户提供了 Windows 等专有桌面之外的免费替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KDE: The Documentary tells the story of the open-source community that emerged in 1996 to offer computer users a free alternative to proprietary desktops such as Windows. It traces the origins and development of KDE as a pioneering desktop project.</span><span class='zh'>《KDE：纪录片》讲述了这一开源社区的故事：它于 1996 年兴起，为计算机用户提供了 Windows 等专有桌面之外的免费替代方案。影片追溯了 KDE 这一先驱桌面项目的起源与发展。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Hydrogen as a fast, cost-efficient model for agentic workflows and everyday tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，将其定位为面向智能体工作流和日常任务的快速、高性价比模型。</span></p>

Google also says Hydrogen brings advanced intelligence to users broadly.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，将其定位为面向智能体工作流和日常任务的快速、高性价比模型。公司将其定位为可日常使用、适合可靠智能体工作的模型。Google 还表示，Hydrogen 将先进的智能能力带给更广泛的用户。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Frame is a handmade wooden frame containing a 31.5-inch full-color ePaper display.</span><span class='zh'>Frame 是一款采用手工木制外壳的 31.5 英寸全彩电子纸显示器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frame is a handmade wooden frame containing a 31.5-inch full-color ePaper display. It is the product’s third release and is now available for pre-order. The first batch is limited to about 470 units, with a few intended to ship by the end of 2026 and most deliveries expected in early 2027.</span><span class='zh'>Frame 是一款采用手工木制外壳的 31.5 英寸全彩电子纸显示器。这是该产品的第三次发布，目前已开放预订。首批约 470 台，计划在 2026 年底前发出少量产品，大多数订单预计于 2027 年初交付。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://macanorak.com/uk-government-forced-to-admit-it-issued-apple-a-secret-encryption-order/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://macanorak.com/content/images/2026/11/Apple.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='macanorak.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">47</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://macanorak.com/uk-government-forced-to-admit-it-issued-apple-a-secret-encryption-order/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UK government forced to admit it issued Apple a secret encryption order</span><span class='zh'>英国政府被迫承认曾向苹果发出秘密加密命令</span></a><span class="dom">macanorak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Investigatory Powers Tribunal rejected the UK government’s attempt to keep its order to Apple secret.</span><span class='zh'>调查权力法庭驳回了英国政府试图对苹果命令保持保密的做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Investigatory Powers Tribunal rejected the UK government’s attempt to keep its order to Apple secret. The tribunal ruled that the government must acknowledge issuing Apple a Technical Capability Notice under Section 253 of the Investigatory Powers Act 2016. Such notices can require technology companies to alter services to facilitate investigatory powers, in this case amid a dispute over encrypted iCloud data.</span><span class='zh'>调查权力法庭驳回了英国政府试图对苹果命令保持保密的做法。法庭裁定，政府必须承认其依据《2016 年调查权力法》第 253 条向苹果发出过技术能力通知。这类通知可以要求科技公司修改服务，以协助行使调查权；本案涉及加密 iCloud 数据争议。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>6.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>513</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562113' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>218</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50506719" data-id="50506719" data-tags="Hardware" data-score="504" data-comments="283" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50506719" data-time="1794748522">
<a class="thumb" href="https://macanorak.com/the-macbook-pros-oled-pwm-problem-when-a-better-display-becomes-worse-to-use/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://macanorak.com/content/images/2026/11/PROLED-Display-XDR-1.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='macanorak.com' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">48</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://macanorak.com/the-macbook-pros-oled-pwm-problem-when-a-better-display-becomes-worse-to-use/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The MacBook Pro&#x27;s OLED PWM problem</span><span class='zh'>MacBook Pro 的 OLED PWM 问题：更好的显示屏为何更难用</span></a><span class="dom">macanorak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has introduced tandem OLED technology to the MacBook Pro, alongside the Mini-LED option that remains available.</span><span class='zh'>苹果为 MacBook Pro 引入了串联 OLED 技术，同时继续提供 Mini-LED 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has introduced tandem OLED technology to the MacBook Pro, alongside the Mini-LED option that remains available. The display offers individually illuminated pixels, effectively infinite contrast, deeper blacks, higher peak HDR brightness, and touch input, prompting an overwhelmingly positive initial reaction. The source argues that impressive image quality does not necessarily make the screen comfortable for every user, particularly because of its PWM behavior.</span><span class='zh'>苹果为 MacBook Pro 引入了串联 OLED 技术，同时继续提供 Mini-LED 版本。该显示屏具备独立发光像素、近乎无限的对比度、更深的黑色、更高的 HDR 峰值亮度，并首次支持触控，发布初期反响普遍积极。文章指出，出色的画质并不一定意味着所有用户都能舒适使用，尤其是其 PWM 特性可能带来问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>504</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50506719' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>283</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50472260" data-id="50472260" data-tags="AI" data-score="495" data-comments="161" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50472260" data-time="1794509521">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://antigravity.google/blog/gemini-4-hydrogen-in-google-antigravity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen and Orchestrator</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen 与 Orchestrator</span></a><span class="dom">antigravity.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google Antigravity is rolling out Gemini 4 Hydrogen together with a new model-selector option called Orchestrator.</span><span class='zh'>Google Antigravity 正在推出 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，以及模型选择器中的新选项 Orchestrator。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google Antigravity is rolling out Gemini 4 Hydrogen together with a new model-selector option called Orchestrator. Orchestrator pairs Hydrogen with Google’s strongest models for long-horizon tasks, including Gemini 4 Carbon. The combination is intended to balance quality, efficiency, and latency.</span><span class='zh'>Google Antigravity 正在推出 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，以及模型选择器中的新选项 Orchestrator。Orchestrator 会将 Hydrogen 与包括 Gemini 4 Carbon 在内的 Google 最强模型搭配，用于长时程任务。该组合旨在平衡质量、效率和延迟。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://consumerrights.wiki/w/ID.me_terms_required_for_IRS_sign-in" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ID.me terms required for IRS sign-in</span><span class='zh'>IRS 登录要求接受 ID.me 条款</span></a><span class="dom">consumerrights.wiki</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Taxpayers using ID.me to sign in to IRS online services accept terms that send most disputes to arbitration rather than a judge or jury, unless they opt out within 30 days.</span><span class='zh'>纳税人使用 ID.me 登录美国国税局在线服务时，将接受把大多数争议提交仲裁、而非交由法官或陪审团审理的条款，除非在 30 天内选择退出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Taxpayers using ID.me to sign in to IRS online services accept terms that send most disputes to arbitration rather than a judge or jury, unless they opt out within 30 days. ID.me&#x27;s privacy policy says it may use information for marketing and transfer information during a sale, merger, or similar transaction. It excludes government identity-verification data from ID.me Shop marketing unless the user consents or another listed exception applies. Login.gov and IRS Direct Pay remain alternatives for IRS access.</span><span class='zh'>纳税人使用 ID.me 登录美国国税局在线服务时，将接受把大多数争议提交仲裁、而非交由法官或陪审团审理的条款，除非在 30 天内选择退出。ID.me 的隐私政策称，其可能将信息用于营销，并在出售、合并或类似交易中转移信息。政府身份验证数据通常不会用于 ID.me Shop 的营销，但用户同意或符合其他列明例外的情况除外。IRS 仍提供 Login.gov 和无需登录的 Direct Pay 作为替代方式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>492</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50540437' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>259</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50502988" data-id="50502988" data-tags="Legal|Business" data-score="492" data-comments="219" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50502988" data-time="1794705516">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://litnews.ai/articles/garciaguirre-samsung-sk-hynix-micron-dram-conspiracy-suit-survives-dismissal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Court denies motion to dismiss DRAM price fixing case</span><span class='zh'>法院驳回 DRAM 价格操纵案的撤诉申请</span></a><span class="dom">litnews.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A federal judge refused to dismiss a proposed class action accusing Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of conspiring to restrict conventional DRAM supply and shift capacity toward HBM.</span><span class='zh'>一名联邦法官拒绝驳回一项拟议集体诉讼，该诉讼指控三星、SK 海力士和美光合谋限制传统 DRAM 供应，并将产能转向 HBM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A federal judge refused to dismiss a proposed class action accusing Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of conspiring to restrict conventional DRAM supply and shift capacity toward HBM. Judge Noël Wise found that buyers had plausibly alleged an agreement violating Section 1 of the Sherman Act. The allegations have been accepted only for this procedural stage and have not been proven.</span><span class='zh'>一名联邦法官拒绝驳回一项拟议集体诉讼，该诉讼指控三星、SK 海力士和美光合谋限制传统 DRAM 供应，并将产能转向 HBM。Noël Wise 法官认定，买方对违反《谢尔曼法》第 1 条的合谋提出了具有可信性的指控。目前这些指控仅在这一程序阶段被视为成立，并未得到证实。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>492</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50502988' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>219</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50547777" data-id="50547777" data-tags="AI" data-score="484" data-comments="251" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547777" data-time="1795016974">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://europeanbusinessmagazine.com/ai-agents-better-questions-kpmg/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI agents ask better questions than KPMG accountants in audit trial</span><span class='zh'>审计试验中，AI 智能体比 KPMG 会计师提出了更好的问题</span></a><span class="dom">europeanbusinessmagazine.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KPMG UK tested more than 100 AI agents on the accounts of a major UK-listed company.</span><span class='zh'>KPMG 英国分部让 100 多个 AI 智能体审查一家英国上市大型公司的账目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KPMG UK tested more than 100 AI agents on the accounts of a major UK-listed company. The agents sometimes investigated financial issues more deeply and asked better questions than the human audit team. The experiment raises questions about how automation could change the economics of auditing as machines take on more analytical work.</span><span class='zh'>KPMG 英国分部让 100 多个 AI 智能体审查一家英国上市大型公司的账目。在试验中，智能体有时比人类审计团队更深入地调查财务问题，并提出更好的问题。随着机器承担更多分析工作，这项试验引发了人们对自动化将如何改变审计行业经济模式的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ounapuu.ee/posts/2026/11/19/unsafeguards/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fuck Your AI Safeguards</span><span class='zh'>去你的 AI 安全护栏</span></a><span class="dom">ounapuu.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer describes asking Claude Code to identify easy security improvements in a project and finding a one-line fix.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者让 Claude Code 找出项目中容易修复的安全问题，并发现了一个只需一行代码即可解决的问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer describes asking Claude Code to identify easy security improvements in a project and finding a one-line fix. The attempt was then blocked by AI safeguards, despite the developer saying they controlled the product and were trying to address security and compliance issues.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者让 Claude Code 找出项目中容易修复的安全问题，并发现了一个只需一行代码即可解决的问题。随后，这一操作被 AI 安全护栏阻止，尽管开发者表示自己控制着该产品，目的是修复安全问题并满足合规要求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>482</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562262' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>194</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50496602" data-id="50496602" data-tags="Privacy|AI" data-score="468" data-comments="175" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496602" data-time="1794660345">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/tech-companies-are-trying-shift-entire-concept-privacy-again" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/mobile-privacy.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/tech-companies-are-trying-shift-entire-concept-privacy-again" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tech companies are trying to shift the concept of privacy again</span><span class='zh'>科技公司又在试图改变隐私的定义</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Privacy is increasingly affecting people beyond the individual who owns or uses a device.</span><span class='zh'>隐私问题正越来越多地影响设备所有者或使用者之外的人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Privacy is increasingly affecting people beyond the individual who owns or uses a device. Meta and Google’s smart glasses can record what the wearer is looking at or use AI to describe it, extending the consequences of data collection to bystanders. The shift challenges the older framing of privacy as primarily a matter of personal choice.</span><span class='zh'>隐私问题正越来越多地影响设备所有者或使用者之外的人。Meta 和 Google 的智能眼镜可以记录佩戴者所看到的内容，或使用 AI 对其进行描述，使数据收集的后果波及旁观者。这种变化挑战了过去主要将隐私视为个人选择的传统定义。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">57</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://monkeyisland.pages.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Monkey Island: all 13 versions side by side</span><span class='zh'>《猴岛的秘密》全部 13 个版本并排对比</span></a><span class="dom">monkeyisland.pages.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project presents every version of The Secret of Monkey Island from 1990 side by side, covering eight editions and 13 display modes across systems including Hercules, CGA, EGA, VGA, Amiga, Atari ST, Macintosh, FM Towns, and Sega CD.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目并排展示了 1990 年《猴岛的秘密》的所有版本，涵盖 Hercules、CGA、EGA、VGA、Amiga、Atari ST、Macintosh、FM Towns 和 Sega CD 等平台上的 8 个版本、13 种显示模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project presents every version of The Secret of Monkey Island from 1990 side by side, covering eight editions and 13 display modes across systems including Hercules, CGA, EGA, VGA, Amiga, Atari ST, Macintosh, FM Towns, and Sega CD. It shows the same 12 scenes in each version, with captures scaled to 1600×1200 using nearest-neighbor scaling and corrected for a 4:3 display. The presentation keeps the framing constant so differences come from the original machines and display modes.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目并排展示了 1990 年《猴岛的秘密》的所有版本，涵盖 Hercules、CGA、EGA、VGA、Amiga、Atari ST、Macintosh、FM Towns 和 Sega CD 等平台上的 8 个版本、13 种显示模式。每个版本都展示相同的 12 个场景，截图使用最近邻算法放大到 1600×1200，并进行 4:3 显示比例校正。展示保持相同构图，因此差异主要来自原始机器和显示模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m making LLMs work on small and old devices</span><span class='zh'>让大语言模型运行在小型和老旧设备上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers. It uses custom kernels, memory optimization, and weight streaming, with claims measured on the actual hardware. On a Xiaomi TV Box S 3rd Gen with about 1 GB of usable RAM, the project reports running models ranging from 260K to 16B parameters.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。项目使用定制内核、内存优化和权重流式传输，并在真实硬件上测量各项结果。在可用内存约 1 GB 的 Xiaomi TV Box S 第三代上，项目报告称可运行参数量从 26 万到 160 亿不等的模型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://steveblank.com/2026/11/15/whats-next-for-nvidia/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What&#x27;s Next for Nvidia?</span><span class='zh'>英伟达的下一步是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">steveblank.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board announced that co-founder Jensen Huang had resigned after 33 years at the company.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会宣布，联合创始人黄仁勋在公司任职 33 年后辞职。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board announced that co-founder Jensen Huang had resigned after 33 years at the company. The change raises questions about what happens when a company loses a visionary chief executive. The supplied discussion argues that Nvidia could risk losing innovation and relying on momentum and its brand.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会宣布，联合创始人黄仁勋在公司任职 33 年后辞职。这一变动引发了人们对公司失去具有远见的首席执行官后将如何发展的疑问。相关讨论认为，英伟达可能面临创新减弱、转而依赖既有势头和品牌的风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/MSFT365Status/status/2121079061774491735" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft Outlook/365 still down after 12 hours</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft Outlook/365 持续中断 12 小时后仍未恢复</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft said changes intended to improve serviceability were not producing the expected results during the Outlook/365 outage.</span><span class='zh'>微软表示，在 Outlook/365 宕机期间，为改善服务能力而实施的变更没有达到预期效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft said changes intended to improve serviceability were not producing the expected results during the Outlook/365 outage. The company was identifying alternative workstreams to increase capacity amid sustained traffic-management imbalances. The service had remained disrupted for more than 12 hours.</span><span class='zh'>微软表示，在 Outlook/365 宕机期间，为改善服务能力而实施的变更没有达到预期效果。面对持续的流量管理失衡，公司正在寻找其他工作方案以提升容量。服务中断当时已持续超过 12 个小时。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phrack.org/issues/73/introduction_md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Phrack 73</span><span class='zh'>Phrack 73</span></a><span class="dom">phrack.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Phrack&#x27;s latest release follows its 40th-anniversary relaunch, which included a printed edition, conference events, talks, and a capture-the-flag challenge.</span><span class='zh'>Phrack 的最新一期延续了其 40 周年重启活动，该活动包括纸质版、会议活动、演讲和夺旗挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Phrack&#x27;s latest release follows its 40th-anniversary relaunch, which included a printed edition, conference events, talks, and a capture-the-flag challenge. The relaunch aimed to put new research in readers&#x27; hands and encourage people to read, research, write, and contribute again. Its stated goal is to inspire readers to take systems apart and investigate how they work.</span><span class='zh'>Phrack 的最新一期延续了其 40 周年重启活动，该活动包括纸质版、会议活动、演讲和夺旗挑战。重启的目标是把新的研究成果交到读者手中，并鼓励人们重新阅读、研究、写作和贡献。其明确目标是激发读者拆解系统、探究其工作方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ductus.sorenblank.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Handwriting, as a Font</span><span class='zh'>把手写字迹变成字体</span></a><span class="dom">ductus.sorenblank.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This browser tool turns a user’s handwriting into a font.</span><span class='zh'>这个浏览器工具可以将用户的手写字迹转换为字体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This browser tool turns a user’s handwriting into a font. Users write each letter in the browser and download the result as OTF or WOFF2. No account or upload is required, and the letters remain on the device.</span><span class='zh'>这个浏览器工具可以将用户的手写字迹转换为字体。用户在浏览器中书写每个字母，然后下载 OTF 或 WOFF2 格式的字体。它无需账户或上传，字母会保留在设备上。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bruceroettgers.eu/blog/protomaps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protomaps: Self-hosting map tiles has gotten cheap and easy</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps：自托管地图瓦片如今既便宜又简单</span></a><span class="dom">bruceroettgers.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Protomaps uses PMTiles, a tile format that stores a map in a single file and serves it through HTTP Range requests.</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps 使用 PMTiles，这种瓦片格式将地图存储在单个文件中，并通过 HTTP Range 请求提供服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Protomaps uses PMTiles, a tile format that stores a map in a single file and serves it through HTTP Range requests. The file can be uploaded to a storage bucket and cached by compatible CDNs, while an edge function can translate conventional x/y/z tile URLs for services such as Cloudflare. This makes map data portable and inexpensive to deploy compared with usage-priced commercial APIs.</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps 使用 PMTiles，这种瓦片格式将地图存储在单个文件中，并通过 HTTP Range 请求提供服务。该文件可以上传到存储桶并由支持相关缓存的 CDN 缓存；对于 Cloudflare 等服务，也可以用边缘函数将传统的 x/y/z 瓦片 URL 转换为 Range 请求。与按使用量收费的商业 API 相比，这让地图数据更易移植，也更便宜易部署。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/saas/2026/11/16/microsoft-blames-monster-email-outage-on-software-defect-introduced-in-july-fix/5310026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft blames monster email outage on software defect introduced in July fix</span><span class='zh'>微软称大规模电子邮件故障源于 7 月修复引入的软件缺陷</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft blamed last week’s major Microsoft 365 email outage on a defect introduced by a July 2026 change.</span><span class='zh'>微软将上周 Microsoft 365 大规模电子邮件故障归因于 2026 年 7 月一次变更引入的软件缺陷。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft blamed last week’s major Microsoft 365 email outage on a defect introduced by a July 2026 change. The defect remained dormant in network gateway servers until increased load and reduced capacity triggered it, causing a day-long disruption. Microsoft’s preliminary post-incident report said the change had passed load testing and initially caused no impact after deployment.</span><span class='zh'>微软将上周 Microsoft 365 大规模电子邮件故障归因于 2026 年 7 月一次变更引入的软件缺陷。该缺陷一直潜伏在网络网关服务器中，直到负载上升、容量下降时被触发，造成持续一天的中断。微软的初步事后报告称，该变更通过了负载测试，部署后最初也没有造成影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A software job-title family tree traces how similar work has been labeled over time, from DevOps to SRE and platform engineering.</span><span class='zh'>一份软件岗位名称家谱追溯了相似工作如何随着时间更名，从 DevOps 到 SRE，再到平台工程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://burntsushi.net/jiff/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0: A datetime library for Rust</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0：Rust 的日期时间库</span></a><span class="dom">burntsushi.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0 is a datetime library for Rust that puts time-zone handling at the center of its API.</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0 是一个 Rust 日期时间库，其 API 将时区处理置于核心位置。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0 is a datetime library for Rust that puts time-zone handling at the center of its API. It automatically addresses many common daylight-saving-time problems and is already widely adopted in the Rust ecosystem. The name is pronounced like “gif,” with a soft “g,” as in “gem.”</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0 是一个 Rust 日期时间库，其 API 将时区处理置于核心位置。它会自动处理许多与夏令时有关的常见问题，并已在 Rust 生态中得到广泛采用。Jiff 的发音类似带软 g 音的“gif”，就像“gem”中的 g。</span></p>
