<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6eeccf216579fe2181ae737d30f31abd7590125c/210_58_1759_1407/master/1759.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=334efc63d7472a97d72be0ffd23af8bb' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theguardian.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">72</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI unlocks Gödel&#x27;s notebooks, said to contain hundreds of proved theorems</span><span class='zh'>AI 解读哥德尔笔记本，据称其中包含数百个已证明定理</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kurt Gödel left 1,009 pages of densely written material, much of it in an obsolete shorthand, and the notebooks are said to contain hundreds of proved theorems.</span><span class='zh'>库尔特·哥德尔留下了 1,009 页密集书写的材料，其中许多使用已过时的速记，据称笔记本里包含数百个已证明定理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kurt Gödel left 1,009 pages of densely written material, much of it in an obsolete shorthand, and the notebooks are said to contain hundreds of proved theorems. Gödel’s published incompleteness theorems transformed mathematics and helped pave the way for the digital age. His unpublished work spans tens of thousands of pages and remains of interest to historians of science.</span><span class='zh'>库尔特·哥德尔留下了 1,009 页密集书写的材料，其中许多使用已过时的速记，据称笔记本里包含数百个已证明定理。哥德尔发表的不完备性定理改变了数学，并为数字时代铺平了道路。他未发表的作品多达数万页，至今仍受到科学史学者关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/anthropic-prices-initial-public-offering" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic prices initial public offering</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 确定 IPO 发行价</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic priced its initial public offering at $83 per Class A share.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 将其 A 类普通股 IPO 发行价定为每股 83 美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic priced its initial public offering at $83 per Class A share. The offering consists of 980 million shares, including 956,351,354 sold by Anthropic and 23,648,646 sold by existing stockholders. Trading is expected to begin on Nasdaq under the symbol “ANT” on November 19, 2026, with closing expected the following day and an underwriter option for up to 147 million additional shares.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 将其 A 类普通股 IPO 发行价定为每股 83 美元。本次发行共 9.8 亿股，其中 Anthropic 出售 956,351,354 股，部分现有股东出售 23,648,646 股。股票预计于 2026 年 11 月 19 日以“ANT”为代码在纳斯达克开始交易，发行预计于次日完成，承销商另拥有最多追加购买 1.47 亿股的 30 天期权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sciencenorway.no/culture-history-history-of-science/350-years-ago-this-man-discovered-that-light-has-a-finite-speed/2737969" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>350 years ago, Ole Rømer discovered that light has a finite speed</span><span class='zh'>350 年前，奥勒·罗默发现光速有限</span></a><span class="dom">sciencenorway.no</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In September 1676, Ole Rømer predicted that Jupiter&#x27;s moon Io would emerge from the planet&#x27;s shadow 10 minutes later than expected.</span><span class='zh'>1676 年 9 月，奥勒·罗默预测木星卫星木卫一从行星阴影中出现的时间会比预期晚 10 分钟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In September 1676, Ole Rømer predicted that Jupiter&#x27;s moon Io would emerge from the planet&#x27;s shadow 10 minutes later than expected. He presented the prediction to members of the Académie des Sciences in Paris. The observation demonstrated that light takes time to travel.</span><span class='zh'>1676 年 9 月，奥勒·罗默预测木星卫星木卫一从行星阴影中出现的时间会比预期晚 10 分钟。他将这一预测提交给巴黎科学院成员。这一观测证明了光的传播需要时间。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='joshka.net' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Terminal borders that don&#x27;t cost a cell</span><span class='zh'>不占用终端单元格的边框</span></a><span class="dom">joshka.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns. The implementation works in Ghostty, with demos built using Ratatui. It aims to give terminal user interfaces more visual design options than conventional box-based layouts.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。该实现可在 Ghostty 中运行，并提供了使用 Ratatui 构建的演示。它旨在为终端用户界面提供比传统方框布局更多的视觉设计选择。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A website owner noticed a new backlink from w2rt6byl5nf1opmc-a.com while demonstrating a backlink monitor to a friend.</span><span class='zh'>一位网站所有者在向朋友演示反向链接监控器时，发现 w2rt6byl5nf1opmc-a.com 突然产生了一个新链接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A website owner noticed a new backlink from w2rt6byl5nf1opmc-a.com while demonstrating a backlink monitor to a friend. After clicking it, the owner remembered that the site could log an IP address or contain a multi-day exploit chain, and was greeted by an unexpected page.</span><span class='zh'>一位网站所有者在向朋友演示反向链接监控器时，发现 w2rt6byl5nf1opmc-a.com 突然产生了一个新链接。点击后，他意识到该网站可能记录 IP 地址，甚至包含持续数日的攻击链，随后看到了一个出乎意料的页面。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ggml v1.0.0</span><span class='zh'>ggml v1.0.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support. It also delivers performance work across CUDA, Metal, Vulkan, SYCL, OpenCL, WebGPU, and Hexagon backends, including quantized matrix multiplication, MoE fusion, FlashAttention paths, and scheduler improvements. The release broadens hardware and backend support while reducing unnecessary input uploads.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。该版本还改进了 CUDA、Metal、Vulkan、SYCL、OpenCL、WebGPU 和 Hexagon 后端的性能，包括量化矩阵乘法、MoE 融合、FlashAttention 路径和调度器优化。此次发布扩大了硬件与后端支持，并减少了不必要的输入上传。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Opening a PowerPoint presentation on an iPad reportedly takes a student 16 clicks.</span><span class='zh'>据称，学生在 iPad 上打开一个 PowerPoint 演示文稿需要点击 16 次。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Opening a PowerPoint presentation on an iPad reportedly takes a student 16 clicks. The item presents this as an example of cumbersome technology use in education.</span><span class='zh'>据称，学生在 iPad 上打开一个 PowerPoint 演示文稿需要点击 16 次。该内容将其作为教育场景中技术操作繁琐的例子。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/claude-black-hole-physics" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://assets.claude.com/57a443b51d13727573b7c1290130d09c5f434b2c.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">80</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/claude-black-hole-physics" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude solves two open problems in the physics of black holes</span><span class='zh'>Claude 破解黑洞物理学中的两个开放问题</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 is reported to have solved two open problems in theoretical physics involving black holes and the search for quantum gravity.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Claude Fable 5.2 解决了理论物理学中的两个开放问题，涉及黑洞和量子引力理论的探索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 is reported to have solved two open problems in theoretical physics involving black holes and the search for quantum gravity. The problems sit at the intersection of general relativity and quantum mechanics, whose mathematical incompatibility remains a central challenge in physics. Researchers often use holographic duality and simplified model universes to study these questions.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Claude Fable 5.2 解决了理论物理学中的两个开放问题，涉及黑洞和量子引力理论的探索。这些问题位于广义相对论与量子力学的交叉地带，而两者在数学上的不相容仍是物理学的核心挑战之一。研究人员经常借助全息对偶和简化的模型宇宙来研究这些问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>391</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50563148' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>180</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50472490" data-id="50472490" data-tags="Space|Science" data-score="387" data-comments="185" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50472490" data-time="1794510333">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/astronomers-found-a-mysterious-object-orbiting-far-beyond-pluto-it-could-be-a-new-dwarf-planet-hidden-in-our-solar-system-180989857/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/4eu4Cs6lY_bS29DXaQHZWCxQBrs=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1024x770:1025x771)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/ab/21/ab21fe86-4d88-46f9-bcc2-d385adaa2ceb/noirlab2626c.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='smithsonianmag.com' data-c='#818cf8' data-tag='Space'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">81</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/astronomers-found-a-mysterious-object-orbiting-far-beyond-pluto-it-could-be-a-new-dwarf-planet-hidden-in-our-solar-system-180989857/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dwarf planet candidate appears which takes roughly 280 years to orbit our sun</span><span class='zh'>一颗矮行星候选体出现了，绕太阳一周约需 280 年</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. It appears unusually large and would take roughly 280 years to orbit the Sun. Its size still needs to be determined before astronomers can classify it as a dwarf planet.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。它看起来异常大，绕太阳一周大约需要 280 年。在将其归类为矮行星之前，天文学家仍需确定它的大小。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://verialabs.com/ruby2shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ruby2ßhell: Pre-authentication RCE on stock Rails</span><span class='zh'>Ruby2ßhell：原生 Rails 上的预认证远程代码执行</span></a><span class="dom">verialabs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ruby2Shell is a one-byte heap overflow in Ruby&#x27;s string case-conversion code.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby2Shell 是 Ruby 字符串大小写转换代码中的单字节堆溢出漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ruby2Shell is a one-byte heap overflow in Ruby&#x27;s string case-conversion code. In common Ruby web frameworks and self-hosted software, the flaw can be reached without authentication when affected configurations or application code process strings in a legacy encoding. The advisory lists popular software whose latest versions bundle an affected Ruby.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby2Shell 是 Ruby 字符串大小写转换代码中的单字节堆溢出漏洞。在常见 Ruby Web 框架和自托管软件中，受影响的默认配置或处理旧式编码字符串的应用代码可能让攻击者无需认证触发该漏洞。公告列出了最新版本仍捆绑受影响 Ruby 的热门软件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kapteko.com/15-million-webservers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>15M web servers on one VM – smoltcp, DPDK, AF_XDP</span><span class='zh'>一台虚拟机运行 1500 万个 Web 服务器——smoltcp、DPDK 与 AF_XDP</span></a><span class="dom">kapteko.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project aims to run 15 million web servers on one virtual machine with 10 GB of RAM and a single CPU core.</span><span class='zh'>该项目的目标是在一台配备 10 GB 内存和单个 CPU 核心的虚拟机上运行 1500 万个 Web 服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project aims to run 15 million web servers on one virtual machine with 10 GB of RAM and a single CPU core. It explores kernel bypass and moving the TCP/IP stack into user space, using technologies such as smoltcp, DPDK, and AF_XDP. The stated goal is to understand how far server density can be pushed, rather than to deploy a conventional web service.</span><span class='zh'>该项目的目标是在一台配备 10 GB 内存和单个 CPU 核心的虚拟机上运行 1500 万个 Web 服务器。项目探索内核旁路以及将 TCP/IP 协议栈移到用户态，并使用 smoltcp、DPDK 和 AF_XDP 等技术。其目标是研究服务器密度的极限，而不是部署传统的 Web 服务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.freightwaves.com/news/11-4-million-in-nvidia-hardware-vanishes-during-shipment-from-california-to-texas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>$11.4M in Nvidia hardware vanishes during shipment from CA to TX</span><span class='zh'>价值 1,140 万美元的英伟达硬件在从加州运往德州途中失踪</span></a><span class="dom">freightwaves.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas. The truck was collected on August 29, made several extended stops, and was last tracked in San Bernardino County. Oakland police confirmed an ongoing grand-theft investigation, while the carrier’s identity and the circumstances remain unclear.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。卡车于 8 月 29 日提货，途中多次长时间停留，最后在圣贝纳迪诺县失去踪迹。奥克兰警方已确认正在调查这起重大盗窃案，但承运方身份和货物失踪经过仍不清楚。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ransomnews.com/burger-king-tweet-bitly-link-hijack-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Old Bitly links are being hijacked</span><span class='zh'>旧 Bitly 链接正遭劫持</span></a><span class="dom">ransomnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Burger King and McDonald’s were not hacked.</span><span class='zh'>汉堡王和麦当劳并未遭到黑客入侵。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Burger King and McDonald’s were not hacked. Instead, someone re-registered Bitly links posted in their 2011 tweets. The same person or group has replaced 57 of 97 links in old tweets by Donald Trump.</span><span class='zh'>汉堡王和麦当劳并未遭到黑客入侵。有人重新注册了它们在 2011 年推文中使用的 Bitly 链接。同样的问题还影响了唐纳德·特朗普旧推文中的 97 个链接，其中 57 个已被替换。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>374</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50511395' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>196</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50558906" data-id="50558906" data-tags="Security|Code" data-score="372" data-comments="95" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558906" data-time="1795088823">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openjdk/jdk/pull/32781" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Someone reported java4shell bug in September 2026</span><span class='zh'>有人在 2026 年 9 月报告了 java4shell 漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An OpenJDK pull request reports a heap-corruption bug in the x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>一份 OpenJDK 拉取请求报告了 x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 解码器中的堆损坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An OpenJDK pull request reports a heap-corruption bug in the x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 decoder. The flaw appears when invalid input leaves a short padded tail, causing the decoder to calculate a negative output size and write beyond the logical output buffer before returning to Java. Valid inputs with lengths that are multiples of four do not trigger the issue.</span><span class='zh'>一份 OpenJDK 拉取请求报告了 x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 解码器中的堆损坏漏洞。当无效输入留下很短的填充尾部时，解码器可能计算出负的输出大小，并在返回 Java 之前写出逻辑输出缓冲区边界。长度为 4 的倍数的有效输入不会触发该问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>372</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558906' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>95</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50537156" data-id="50537156" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="372" data-comments="199" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50537156" data-time="1794945040">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.solidjs.com/blog/solid-2-0-the-long-hold/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solid 2.0</span><span class='zh'>Solid 2.0</span></a><span class="dom">solidjs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solid 2.0 has reached a stable release after five years of development.</span><span class='zh'>Solid 2.0 在经过五年开发后达到稳定版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solid 2.0 has reached a stable release after five years of development. It adds asynchronous work in the reactive graph, server functions, agent-oriented diagnostics, and a smaller API. Solid&#x27;s original fine-grained, signal-based approach has since become more common across frontend frameworks. The release builds on that model while simplifying the framework&#x27;s interface.</span><span class='zh'>Solid 2.0 在经过五年开发后达到稳定版本。它加入了响应式图中的异步能力、服务器函数、面向代理的诊断工具，并缩小了 API。Solid 最初采用的细粒度信号式方案如今已在多个前端框架中变得普遍。此次发布在延续这一模型的同时简化了框架接口。</span></p>

After setup, it works offline on Mac, Windows, and Linux.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个免费的开源生存资料库，包含本地 AI、地图和操作教学视频。完成设置后，它可在 Mac、Windows 和 Linux 上离线运行。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=ice-wm.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>ice-wm.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">90</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IceWM</span><span class='zh'>IceWM</span></a><span class="dom">ice-wm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System. It provides a taskbar, keyboard and mouse controls, dynamic menus, multiple-monitor support through RandR and Xinerama, themes, and optional session and wallpaper managers. Created by Marko Maček in 1997 using C++, it is now maintained on GitHub and is available on many Linux distributions and BSD systems.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。它提供任务栏、键盘和鼠标控制、动态菜单，以及通过 RandR 和 Xinerama 实现的多显示器支持，还支持主题和可选的会话、壁纸管理器。IceWM 由 Marko Maček 于 1997 年使用 C++ 创建，目前在 GitHub 上维护，并可用于多种 Linux 发行版和 BSD 系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>356</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>217</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50547344" data-id="50547344" data-tags="Politics" data-score="351" data-comments="162" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547344" data-time="1795015110">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">91</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.yahoo.com/news/world/articles/russia-reportedly-planned-mass-casualty-090500574.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Russia reportedly planned mass-casualty attack in Poland to be blamed on Ukraine</span><span class='zh'>据报道，俄罗斯曾策划在波兰制造大规模伤亡并嫁祸乌克兰</span></a><span class="dom">yahoo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Russian intelligence services reportedly planned a sabotage operation on Polish territory near the border with Russia’s Kaliningrad Oblast.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，俄罗斯情报部门曾计划在波兰境内、靠近俄罗斯加里宁格勒州边境的地区实施破坏行动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Russian intelligence services reportedly planned a sabotage operation on Polish territory near the border with Russia’s Kaliningrad Oblast. The operation was reportedly intended to cause mass casualties in late October or early November and be blamed on Ukraine. The report comes from the Polish newspaper Rzeczpospolita, as cited by European Pravda.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，俄罗斯情报部门曾计划在波兰境内、靠近俄罗斯加里宁格勒州边境的地区实施破坏行动。据称，该行动计划于 10 月下旬或 11 月上旬制造大规模伤亡，并将责任嫁祸给乌克兰。相关消息来自波兰报纸《共和国报》，由 European Pravda 引述。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>351</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547344' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>162</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532086" data-id="50532086" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Programming|DevOps|Energy" data-score="344" data-comments="234" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532086" data-time="1794926736">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jordankrueger.com/blog/better-open-source-not-more-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jordankrueger.com/images/gallery/blog-better-open-source-not-more-apps.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jordankrueger.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">92</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jordankrueger.com/blog/better-open-source-not-more-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We don&#x27;t need more apps, we need better open source</span><span class='zh'>我们不需要更多应用，而需要更好的开源软件</span></a><span class="dom">jordankrueger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI has made it easy to build small applications over a weekend, such as habit trackers, to-do lists, and note-taking tools.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能让人们可以在一个周末轻松构建习惯追踪器、待办事项和笔记应用等小型软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI has made it easy to build small applications over a weekend, such as habit trackers, to-do lists, and note-taking tools. The author argues that more of this energy should go toward improving the open-source software people already rely on, making it faster, lighter, and easier to run. The point is not to discourage people from building, but to redirect some of that effort toward shared infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能让人们可以在一个周末轻松构建习惯追踪器、待办事项和笔记应用等小型软件。作者认为，更多这类精力应投入到改进人们已经依赖的开源软件上，让它们更快、更轻量、更易运行。重点不是阻止人们开发新东西，而是将部分努力转向共享基础设施。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>344</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532086' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>234</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50463399" data-id="50463399" data-tags="AI|Programming|Hardware" data-score="341" data-comments="172" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50463399" data-time="1794461364">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex were used to reverse engineer the ECU of a 1994 Jeep.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 被用于逆向工程一辆 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex were used to reverse engineer the ECU of a 1994 Jeep. The project used a $3 Arduino as part of the setup. It is presented as a video demonstration of AI-assisted automotive reverse engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 被用于逆向工程一辆 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU。该项目使用了一块 3 美元的 Arduino 作为设备的一部分。相关成果以视频形式展示了 AI 辅助的汽车系统逆向工程过程。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">94</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.breck-mckye.com/blog/2026/11/software-is-breaking-down-at-a-systemic-level/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Software is breaking down at a systemic level</span><span class='zh'>软件正在系统性崩坏</span></a><span class="dom">breck-mckye.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that recent failures point to software problems at a systemic level.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，近期的多起故障表明软件正面临系统性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that recent failures point to software problems at a systemic level. The examples cited are a two-day global Microsoft outage and an FAA ground stop that halted all flights in the United States. These events are presented as a recent combination of major technology and infrastructure disruptions.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，近期的多起故障表明软件正面临系统性问题。文章举出的例子包括持续两天的全球微软服务中断，以及导致美国所有航班停飞的 FAA 地面停飞令。这些事件被视为近期重大技术和基础设施中断的集中体现。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>340</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50520866' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>218</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50503255" data-id="50503255" data-tags="Privacy|Legal" data-score="338" data-comments="122" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503255" data-time="1794708054">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/were-asking-appeals-court-require-warrant-alpr-searches" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/siren_v._san_jose.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">95</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/were-asking-appeals-court-require-warrant-alpr-searches" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re Asking an Appeals Court to Require a Warrant for ALPR Searches</span><span class='zh'>我们请求上诉法院要求 ALPR 搜索取得搜查令</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The request asks an appeals court to require a warrant for searches using automated license plate readers.</span><span class='zh'>该请求要求上诉法院规定，使用自动车牌识别系统进行搜索必须取得搜查令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The request asks an appeals court to require a warrant for searches using automated license plate readers. The argument is that ALPR systems can let law enforcement review a person&#x27;s past movements, rather than merely observe one license plate on a passing car. Their ability to search historical location records is therefore presented as materially different from ordinary roadside observation.</span><span class='zh'>该请求要求上诉法院规定，使用自动车牌识别系统进行搜索必须取得搜查令。其理由是，ALPR 能让执法部门查看某人的历史移动轨迹，而不仅是观察一辆经过车辆的单个车牌。因而，搜索历史位置记录的能力被认为与普通路边观察存在实质差异。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503255' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>122</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50475713" data-id="50475713" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="338" data-comments="104" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50475713" data-time="1794524777">
<a class="thumb" href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.hashnode.com/uploads/covers/6aea03219fa4aa99923b5acf/de5ebdea-3436-4b9a-b3f2-e9910f3a0abc.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='edge0.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">96</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Edge0-145B: Frontier AI Capabilities on Your Own Computer</span><span class='zh'>Edge0-145B：在自己的电脑上运行前沿 AI 能力</span></a><span class="dom">edge0.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools. Edge0-145B is described as an open-source, on-device model with 145 billion parameters for coding, reasoning, and agent tasks. Local inference avoids cloud-model token fees, but large models still require substantial memory, bandwidth, and power.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。Edge0-145B 被称为首个开源的 1450 亿参数设备端模型，可用于编程、推理和智能体任务。本地推理无需支付云模型的 token 费用，但大型模型仍需要大量内存、带宽和电力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50475713' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>104</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50554038" data-id="50554038" data-tags="Security|Programming" data-score="337" data-comments="129" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554038" data-time="1795045970">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.ruby-lang.org/en/news/2026/11/18/multiple-vulnerabilities-in-regexp-and-string/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.ruby-lang.org/images/og-image.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ruby-lang.org' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ruby-lang.org/en/news/2026/11/18/multiple-vulnerabilities-in-regexp-and-string/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Multiple vulnerabilities in Regexp and String case conversion</span><span class='zh'>Regexp 和字符串大小写转换中的多个漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">ruby-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Seven vulnerabilities have been found in Ruby’s regular expression engine and string case-conversion functionality.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby 的正则表达式引擎和字符串大小写转换功能中发现了七个漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Seven vulnerabilities have been found in Ruby’s regular expression engine and string case-conversion functionality. Proofs of concept show that some of them can trigger remote code execution. Ruby released versions 4.0.10, 3.4.13, and 3.3.14 to fix the issues and recommends upgrading; CVE identifiers have not yet been assigned.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby 的正则表达式引擎和字符串大小写转换功能中发现了七个漏洞。概念验证表明，其中一些漏洞可用于触发远程代码执行。Ruby 已发布 4.0.10、3.4.13 和 3.3.14 修复这些问题，并建议用户升级；这些漏洞尚未分配 CVE 编号。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://runta.com/blog/introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What is the best harness for each model?</span><span class='zh'>每种模型最适合什么 harness？</span></a><span class="dom">runta.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 tested 22 coding-agent harnesses with five models across the same 62 tasks, repeating each test four times for 27,280 runs.</span><span class='zh'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 使用 5 个模型，在相同的 62 项任务上测试了 22 个编码代理 harness，每项测试重复 4 次，共运行 27,280 次。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 tested 22 coding-agent harnesses with five models across the same 62 tasks, repeating each test four times for 27,280 runs. The best-value harness varied by model, and cost rankings also shifted substantially between models. With the model held constant, the most expensive harness cost 1.8 to 5 times more per task than the cheapest. The results suggest that changing models may also require reconsidering the harness.</span><span class='zh'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 使用 5 个模型，在相同的 62 项任务上测试了 22 个编码代理 harness，每项测试重复 4 次，共运行 27,280 次。性价比最高的 harness 因模型而异，成本排名也会随模型大幅变化。在固定模型的情况下，最贵 harness 的单任务成本是最便宜者的 1.8 至 5 倍。结果表明，更换模型时可能也需要重新评估所用的 harness。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vaibhaav-Tiwari/deepseek-v3-jax" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DeepSeek V3 in ~200 lines of pure Jax</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用约 200 行纯 JAX 实现 DeepSeek V3</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction. The model was pretrained from scratch on a MacBook Air using public-domain detective fiction, without PyTorch, Flax, or Equinox. Its parameters are plain dictionaries of arrays, and the implementation is organized as readable functions.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。该模型在 MacBook Air 上使用公版侦探小说从头预训练，未使用 PyTorch、Flax 或 Equinox。模型参数采用由数组组成的普通字典，代码则组织为可从头读到尾的函数。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lonewolf-im/lonewolf/releases/tag/v0.3.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3 – A standalone XMPP server written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3：用 Rust 编写的独立 XMPP 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features. It remains a pre-1.0, single-node server, while full XMPP compliance and clustering are still on the roadmap. The release expands secure federation without yet providing those larger deployment features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。它仍是一个处于 1.0 之前阶段的单节点服务器，完整 XMPP 兼容性和集群支持仍在路线图中。此次更新增强了安全联邦能力，但尚未提供这些更大规模部署功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/IRS-Public/direct-file" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US IRS free online tax filing open source repo (2025)</span><span class='zh'>美国国税局免费在线报税开源仓库（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS. It uses interview-style questions, works across phones and computers, supports English and Spanish, and translates users&#x27; answers into tax forms submitted through the IRS Modernized e-File API. The repository contains the service&#x27;s code and related tests and scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。该服务采用问答式界面，可在手机和电脑等设备上使用，支持英语和西班牙语，并将用户答案转换为税表后通过国税局 Modernized e-File API 提交。该开源仓库包含服务代码以及相关测试和场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/snipedbywifi/terminactory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A terminal-native idle factory game, written in Rust with ratatui</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 Rust 和 ratatui 编写的终端原生放置工厂游戏</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks. It includes basic factory mechanics such as building, deleting, selecting, measuring earnings, and researching unlocks. Additional features include a fictional browser with a wiki and forums, extensive lore, and hooks that display coding-agent responses as in-game “telegrams.”</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。游戏包含建造、删除、选择、收益测算和研究解锁等基本工厂机制。它还加入了带有 Wiki 和论坛的虚构浏览器、大量背景故事，以及将编码代理回复显示为游戏内“电报”的钩子功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>138</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497132' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>28</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50471650" data-id="50471650" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Data|Code" data-score="134" data-comments="37" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471650" data-time="1794507348">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">227</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: AgentRun – open-source, Self-improving Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：AgentRun——开源、自我改进的托管智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents. It includes self-improving agents, web-grounded workflows, sandboxes, computer environments, tools and integrations, and behavioral analytics. AgentRun supports harnesses including Pi, Claude Agents, Codex, and OpenCode, and can be self-hosted or used as a hosted service.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。它包含自我改进智能体、基于网页信息的工作流、沙箱和计算机环境、工具与集成，以及智能体行为分析。AgentRun 支持 Pi、Claude Agents、Codex 和 OpenCode 等 harness，也可自行托管或使用托管版本。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/aryamandarda/tamserve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1453712334/2654889e-5808-485c-a426-4ec795152ca2' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aryamandarda/tamserve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Open-source, real-time object tracking for live video feeds</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：面向实时视频流的开源实时目标跟踪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tamserve runs Meta’s SAM 3 on live camera feeds, detecting named objects and tracking them across multiple cameras.</span><span class='zh'>Tamserve 在实时摄像头画面上运行 Meta 的 SAM 3，可根据名称发现目标，并在多个摄像头之间持续跟踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tamserve runs Meta’s SAM 3 on live camera feeds, detecting named objects and tracking them across multiple cameras. On an RTX 4090, it is reported to be four times faster than SAM 3’s tracker for arbitrary objects while retaining 92% of its accuracy; for people, it can handle 16 cameras concurrently. Its memory use stays around 3.5 GB on a 3,000-frame video, while SAM 3 runs out of memory at frame 423.</span><span class='zh'>Tamserve 在实时摄像头画面上运行 Meta 的 SAM 3，可根据名称发现目标，并在多个摄像头之间持续跟踪。在 RTX 4090 上，对于任意目标，其速度据称是 SAM 3 跟踪器的 4 倍，同时保持 92% 的准确率；针对人员，它可以同时处理 16 个摄像头。处理 3,000 帧视频时，其内存占用保持在约 3.5 GB，而 SAM 3 在第 423 帧就会耗尽内存。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>119</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50523377' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50496787" data-id="50496787" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Web|Data|Education|Code" data-score="112" data-comments="68" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496787" data-time="1794662230">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ZigaoWang/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Zigzag, a native Mac browser for people leaving Arc</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Zigzag——为离开 Arc 的用户打造的原生 Mac 浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai. It aims to provide an Arc-like interface and can import spaces, pins, history, passwords, and cookies from Arc or other browsers. Despite using WebKit, it supports Chrome extensions, including 1Password, Bitwarden, and Tampermonkey. The browser requires no account and claims to have no telemetry or analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。它试图提供类似 Arc 的界面，并可从 Arc 或其他浏览器导入空间、固定项目、历史记录、密码和 Cookie。虽然基于 WebKit，它仍支持 Chrome 扩展，包括 1Password、Bitwarden 和 Tampermonkey。该浏览器无需账户，并声称不包含遥测或分析功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>112</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496787' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>68</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50509416" data-id="50509416" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="110" data-comments="42" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509416" data-time="1794767800">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/eclipse-mosquitto/mosquitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto – An open source MQTT broker</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto：开源 MQTT 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1. It also provides C and C++ client libraries and command-line tools for publishing, subscribing, and administering the broker.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。它还提供 C 和 C++ 客户端库，以及用于发布、订阅和管理代理的命令行工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0xdeadf1sh/T1DMAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I have trained 15 blood glucose forecasting models on my own data</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我用自己的数据训练了 15 个血糖预测模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is a model zoo for forecasting blood glucose levels, trained and tested on the author’s personal data as well as the public Metabonet dataset.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is a model zoo for forecasting blood glucose levels, trained and tested on the author’s personal data as well as the public Metabonet dataset. It includes 15 forecasting models, including models developed by the author. Most of the repository’s code was written with AI assistance.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。集合中包含 15 个预测模型，其中也有作者自行开发的模型。仓库中的大部分代码由 AI 辅助编写。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ages2001/WINUDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Winudf – DVD / BluRay driver for DOS, Windows 1.x – 3x / 95 / 98 / ME</span><span class='zh'>Winudf：适用于 DOS、Windows 1.x–3.x、95、98、ME 的 DVD/Blu-ray 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media. It supports UDF versions 1.02 through 2.60, including DVDs, Blu-ray discs, packet-written CDs, and UDF-formatted USB drives. DOS and Windows 1.0x–3.11 receive drive letters through WINUDF.EXE, while WINUDF.VXD provides a long-name file-system driver for Windows 95, 98, and ME.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。它支持 1.02 至 2.60 版 UDF，涵盖 DVD、蓝光光盘、包写入 CD 以及采用 UDF 格式的 USB 驱动器。DOS 和 Windows 1.0x–3.11 可通过 WINUDF.EXE 获得盘符，Windows 95、98 和 ME 则使用支持长文件名的 WINUDF.VXD 驱动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>95</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50515182' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50497147" data-id="50497147" data-tags="Show HN|Web|Hardware|Code" data-score="87" data-comments="26" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147" data-time="1794665292">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">301</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agweb – a browser for agents that stays out of your way</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Agweb——不妨碍用户操作的代理浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus. Agents control the single window from the shell through an agweb command modeled on Playwright CLI, while users can watch all tabs live in one view. Idle tabs are suspended; the author reports that five open pages used 0% CPU on a Mac, compared with five to seven busy cores from Chrome’s GPU process.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。代理通过类似 Playwright CLI 的 agweb 命令从 shell 控制这个窗口，用户则可以在一个视图中实时查看所有标签页。闲置标签页会被挂起；作者称，在 Mac 上打开五个页面时其 CPU 占用为 0%，而 Chrome 的 GPU 进程会占用五到七个繁忙核心。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>87</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>26</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50562320" data-id="50562320" data-tags="Open Source|Design|Startups|Code" data-score="85" data-comments="68" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562320" data-time="1795106044">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/onlook-dev/onlook/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">303</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/onlook-dev/onlook/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Onlook (YC W25) – An open-source developer tool for designers</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Onlook（YC W25）——面向设计师的开源开发工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Onlook is an open-source desktop app that places a design canvas over a real codebase, allowing designers to work directly in code with an existing agent.</span><span class='zh'>Onlook 是一款开源桌面应用，在真实代码库之上提供设计画布，让设计师可以借助现有代理直接进行代码设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Onlook is an open-source desktop app that places a design canvas over a real codebase, allowing designers to work directly in code with an existing agent. Users can work in multiple simultaneous worktrees running the actual app locally, with changes submitted as reviewable or discardable pull requests. The project says more than 25,000 designers and developers have supported it and over 100 people have contributed.</span><span class='zh'>Onlook 是一款开源桌面应用，在真实代码库之上提供设计画布，让设计师可以借助现有代理直接进行代码设计。用户可以在多个同时运行真实应用的工作树中操作，并将每次修改提交为可审查或可丢弃的拉取请求。项目方称已有超过 25,000 名设计师和开发者支持该项目，贡献者超过 100 人。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>85</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562320' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>68</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460263" data-id="50460263" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="82" data-comments="14" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263" data-time="1794438567">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jjba23/sift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sift: LSP, Lint, Format Lisp, Scheme and Emacs Lisp</span><span class='zh'>Sift：用于 Lisp、Scheme 和 Emacs Lisp 的 LSP、Lint 与格式化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp. It reports malformed Lisp source and rewrites whitespace while preserving comments and read-time forms in their original positions. Formatting an already formatted file produces no further changes, a property asserted across the test suite.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。它会报告格式错误的 Lisp 源码并重写空白，同时保留注释和读取时形式的原始位置。对已经格式化的文件再次格式化不会产生变化，该特性在整个测试套件中都有断言验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>82</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>14</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50519778" data-id="50519778" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Data|Code" data-score="80" data-comments="22" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50519778" data-time="1794848203">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FURSOY/fursoy-cpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Fursoy – Out-of-order RV64IMAFDV core in SystemVerilog (Linux 6.6)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 SystemVerilog 编写、支持乱序执行的 RV64IMAFDV 核心（Linux 6.6）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fursoy is a 64-bit out-of-order superscalar RISC-V processor implemented in SystemVerilog.</span><span class='zh'>Fursoy 是一款用 SystemVerilog 实现的 64 位乱序超标量 RISC-V 处理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fursoy is a 64-bit out-of-order superscalar RISC-V processor implemented in SystemVerilog. It supports RV64IMAFDV with Zba and Zbb extensions, boots mainline Linux 6.6.50 and BusyBox under Sv39 virtual memory, and includes separate machine, supervisor, and user privilege levels. Synthesis in Nangate 45nm reached 500 MHz timing closure across eight core functional blocks.</span><span class='zh'>Fursoy 是一款用 SystemVerilog 实现的 64 位乱序超标量 RISC-V 处理器。它支持带 Zba 和 Zbb 扩展的 RV64IMAFDV，可在 Sv39 虚拟内存下启动主线 Linux 6.6.50 和 BusyBox，并具备机器、监管者和用户三级权限。该设计在 Nangate 45nm 工艺中完成了八个核心功能模块的 500 MHz 时序收敛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/basetenlabs/deepseek-ultrafast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware. It supports a 1-million-token context length and multi-server KV-cache sharing. The project measures decode speed, step time, and acceptance length using a Tetris-generation prompt.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。它支持 100 万 token 的上下文长度和多服务器 KV 缓存共享。项目使用生成俄罗斯方块的提示词测量解码速度、步骤耗时和接受长度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Apakottur/unref" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Unref – A Python dead code finder built on Ruff&#x27;s parser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Unref——基于 Ruff 解析器的 Python 死代码查找器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unref is a Python dead-code finder built with Rust and Ruff&#x27;s parser, created because existing alternatives did not work reliably on the author&#x27;s codebases.</span><span class='zh'>Unref 是一款使用 Rust 和 Ruff 解析器构建的 Python 死代码查找器，起因是现有替代工具无法可靠适配作者的代码库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unref is a Python dead-code finder built with Rust and Ruff&#x27;s parser, created because existing alternatives did not work reliably on the author&#x27;s codebases. It can scan all of CPython in under a second and has found substantial dead code in the author&#x27;s team&#x27;s projects. The tool is intended for low-configuration use and is already being used in continuous integration.</span><span class='zh'>Unref 是一款使用 Rust 和 Ruff 解析器构建的 Python 死代码查找器，起因是现有替代工具无法可靠适配作者的代码库。它能在不到一秒内检查完整的 CPython，并在作者团队的项目中发现了大量死代码。该工具定位于低配置使用，目前已用于持续集成。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>59</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50517410' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>17</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50497746" data-id="50497746" data-tags="Science|Code" data-score="56" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497746" data-time="1794669750">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tom7/noperts/blob/main/septopert.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smallest possible nopert polyhedra: the Septopert</span><span class='zh'>最小的非鲁珀特多面体：Septopert</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Septopert is a convex seven-vertex polyhedron that is not Rupert, meaning it cannot pass through a congruent copy of itself.</span><span class='zh'>Septopert 是一种拥有 7 个顶点、且不是鲁珀特多面体的凸多面体，也就是说它无法穿过与自身全等的另一个多面体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Septopert is a convex seven-vertex polyhedron that is not Rupert, meaning it cannot pass through a congruent copy of itself. Seven vertices is the minimum possible for a non-Rupert polyhedron. The page gives exact statements, Lean-checked proofs, and reproduction instructions for the canonical Septopert.</span><span class='zh'>Septopert 是一种拥有 7 个顶点、且不是鲁珀特多面体的凸多面体，也就是说它无法穿过与自身全等的另一个多面体。7 个顶点是非鲁珀特多面体可能达到的最少数量。页面提供了规范 Septopert 的精确陈述、经 Lean 检查的证明和复现步骤。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>56</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497746' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50503930" data-id="50503930" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="47" data-comments="9" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503930" data-time="1794715751">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Tthanh2k6/turbo-browser-mcp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Turbo-Browser-MCP – Batch forms, DOM deltas and submit guards for MCP</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Turbo-Browser-MCP——为 MCP 提供批量表单、DOM 增量和提交保护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbo-Browser-MCP is a Playwright-based MCP server designed to reduce token usage and multi-turn latency in browser automation.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo-Browser-MCP 是一个基于 Playwright 的 MCP 服务器，旨在降低浏览器自动化中的 token 消耗和多轮交互延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbo-Browser-MCP is a Playwright-based MCP server designed to reduce token usage and multi-turn latency in browser automation. It combines 25–33 fine-grained browser primitives into 10 batched tools, with delta DOM snapshots, React/Shadow DOM/iframe form filling, direct disk uploads, parallel tabs, and network-blocking presets. It also includes a default human-in-the-loop guard before form submission.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo-Browser-MCP 是一个基于 Playwright 的 MCP 服务器，旨在降低浏览器自动化中的 token 消耗和多轮交互延迟。它将 25 至 33 个细粒度浏览器原语整合为 10 个批量工具，并支持 DOM 增量快照、React/Shadow DOM/iframe 表单填写、直接从磁盘上传文件、并行标签页和网络阻断预设。提交表单前，它还默认提供人工介入保护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RunanywhereAI/trnrt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TrnRT – An open-source LLM inference engine for AWS Trainium</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TrnRT——面向 AWS Trainium 的开源 LLM 推理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RunAnywhere open-sourced TrnRT, an inference engine for AWS Trainium that currently serves GLM-5.3 Flash in FP8 on an AWS trn2.48xlarge instance.</span><span class='zh'>RunAnywhere 开源了 TrnRT，这是一个面向 AWS Trainium 的推理引擎，目前可在 AWS trn2.48xlarge 实例上以 FP8 运行 GLM-5.3 Flash。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RunAnywhere open-sourced TrnRT, an inference engine for AWS Trainium that currently serves GLM-5.3 Flash in FP8 on an AWS trn2.48xlarge instance. It can serve two model instances on one box using eight Trainium2 chips and a cache-aware router. On the SemiAnalysis AgentX benchmark, the team reports 3.57 million tokens per minute. The project targets large models with long context windows and aims to improve cost efficiency compared with H100 and H200 deployments.</span><span class='zh'>RunAnywhere 开源了 TrnRT，这是一个面向 AWS Trainium 的推理引擎，目前可在 AWS trn2.48xlarge 实例上以 FP8 运行 GLM-5.3 Flash。它可借助 8 个 Trainium2 芯片和感知缓存的路由器，在一台机器上服务两个模型实例。团队称，其在 SemiAnalysis AgentX 基准上的性能达到每分钟 357 万个 token。该项目面向长上下文大模型，目标是相较 H100 和 H200 部署提升成本效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/plabayo/venndb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: VennDB: append-only in-memory Rust DB for rows queried using bit flags</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：VennDB——用位标志查询行数据的追加式内存 Rust 数据库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VennDB is an append-only, in-memory database written in Rust that queries rows through bit-flag columns.</span><span class='zh'>VennDB 是一个用 Rust 编写的追加式内存数据库，通过位标志列查询行数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VennDB is an append-only, in-memory database written in Rust that queries rows through bit-flag columns. It targets mostly static datasets loaded at startup and queried frequently with simple filters, with an emphasis on speed and compactness. For its limited use cases, the project has few dependencies and is described as faster than a naive implementation or a heavier option such as SQLite.</span><span class='zh'>VennDB 是一个用 Rust 编写的追加式内存数据库，通过位标志列查询行数据。它面向主要在启动时加载、之后频繁使用简单过滤条件查询的静态数据集，重点是速度和紧凑性。在适用范围有限的场景中，该项目依赖很少，并被描述为比朴素实现或 SQLite 等更重量级方案更快。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/n4ze3m/tiny-voca" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 4.5M-parameter TTS model runs 70x real-time on CPU and cost just $10 to train</span><span class='zh'>一款 450 万参数的 TTS 模型在 CPU 上以实时速度 70 倍运行，训练成本仅 10 美元</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tiny-voca is a small English text-to-speech model with two voices, F2 and M2.</span><span class='zh'>Tiny-voca 是一款支持 F2 和 M2 两种声音的轻量级英语文本转语音模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tiny-voca is a small English text-to-speech model with two voices, F2 and M2. Its complete model, including the waveform generator, has 4.5 million parameters and was trained from scratch on about 18 hours of synthetic speech for roughly $10. On four CPU threads, it runs at about 70 times real time.</span><span class='zh'>Tiny-voca 是一款支持 F2 和 M2 两种声音的轻量级英语文本转语音模型。包含波形生成器在内的完整模型有 450 万个参数，使用约 18 小时合成语音从头训练，成本约为 10 美元。在四个 CPU 线程上，它的运行速度约为实时速度的 70 倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/db-branch/db-branch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: db-branch – database branches in seconds for AI agents and CI, via ZFS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：db-branch——通过 ZFS 为 AI 代理和 CI 秒速创建数据库分支</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>db-branch creates copy-on-write branches of large MySQL and PostgreSQL databases in seconds using ZFS clones.</span><span class='zh'>db-branch 使用 ZFS 克隆，在几秒内为大型 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 数据库创建写时复制分支。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>db-branch creates copy-on-write branches of large MySQL and PostgreSQL databases in seconds using ZFS clones. The self-hosted tool can provide isolated databases for migrations, AI agents, or pull requests without copying the underlying data. Branches can be reset or discarded, and the project is licensed under Apache-2.0.</span><span class='zh'>db-branch 使用 ZFS 克隆，在几秒内为大型 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 数据库创建写时复制分支。这款自托管工具无需复制底层数据，就能为迁移、AI 代理或每个拉取请求提供独立数据库。分支可以重置或丢弃，项目采用 Apache-2.0 许可证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>33</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50506940' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50482455" data-id="50482455" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="30" data-comments="3" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50482455" data-time="1794572272">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Christ10-8/reflota" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Reflota – Open-source data recovery for NTFS/FAT disks with phased copy</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Reflota——支持分阶段复制的 NTFS/FAT 开源数据恢复工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files. It reads disks directly: for NTFS, it reconstructs the folder tree from the master file table and directory metadata, while for FAT and exFAT it reads directories and follows cluster chains. It is intended for cases where Windows reports that a disk is unformatted, a folder will not open, or Explorer hangs.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。它会直接读取磁盘：处理 NTFS 时读取主文件表并根据目录元数据重建文件夹树，处理 FAT 和 exFAT 时读取目录并跟踪簇链。当 Windows 报告磁盘未格式化、文件夹无法打开或资源管理器卡死时，该工具可用于尝试恢复数据。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>30</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50482455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50525052" data-id="50525052" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="29" data-comments="11" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50525052" data-time="1794871541">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0nelight/sutra-os" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sutra-OS – A self-hosted ontology platform</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Sutra-OS——自托管本体平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sutra-OS is an open-source, self-hosted ontology platform.</span><span class='zh'>Sutra-OS 是一个开源、自托管的本体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sutra-OS is an open-source, self-hosted ontology platform. It represents data as one owned model with typed and linked entities, server-enforced rules, and numbers traceable to their sources. The project describes its design as “Scarce Units, Typed, Related, Actionable.”</span><span class='zh'>Sutra-OS 是一个开源、自托管的本体平台。它将数据表示为用户拥有的统一模型，其中实体具有类型和关联，修改由服务器规则强制执行，所有数字都能追溯到来源。项目将其设计概括为“稀缺、类型化、关联、可行动”。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>29</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50525052' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>11</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50517352" data-id="50517352" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="20" data-comments="20" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50517352" data-time="1794835313">
