<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTodoApp/OpenTodo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OpenTodo – a self-hosted, local-first task manager in one Go binary</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OpenTodo——单个 Go 二进制文件实现的自托管、本地优先任务管理器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTodo is a free, open-source task manager that can be self-hosted or hosted for users.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTodo 是一款免费开源的任务管理器，用户可以自行部署或交由他人托管。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTodo is a free, open-source task manager that can be self-hosted or hosted for users. It works on phones and computers, supports offline use and shared lists, and keeps users’ data under their control. The project is designed as a local-first alternative to subscription-based task applications.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTodo 是一款免费开源的任务管理器，用户可以自行部署或交由他人托管。它支持手机和电脑、离线使用以及与家人或团队共享列表，并让用户掌控自己的数据。该项目旨在提供一种本地优先的替代方案，减少对订阅制任务应用的依赖。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>20</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50517352' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>20</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50509980" data-id="50509980" data-tags="Security|Programming|Code" data-score="14" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509980" data-time="1794771779">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ymsniper/vault-share" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A live service duress prototype</span><span class='zh'>实时服务型胁迫原型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vault-share is a self-hosted, invite-only encrypted file-sharing application built with Flask.</span><span class='zh'>Vault-share 是一个基于 Flask 的自托管、仅限邀请的加密文件共享应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vault-share is a self-hosted, invite-only encrypted file-sharing application built with Flask. Each file uses its own AES-256-GCM key, which is sealed separately for every authorized reader using HPKE, while uploads are signed and integrity-checked. The prototype also supports duress passwords that reveal separate cover vaults and can require a USB flash drive for the master key.</span><span class='zh'>Vault-share 是一个基于 Flask 的自托管、仅限邀请的加密文件共享应用。每个文件使用独立的 AES-256-GCM 密钥，并通过 HPKE 分别封装给每位获授权的读取者；上传内容还会进行签名和完整性校验。该原型还支持在胁迫密码下打开独立的伪装保险库，并可要求使用 USB 闪存盘保存主密钥。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>14</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509980' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50488901" data-id="50488901" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="10" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50488901" data-time="1794600228">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/agda/agda/releases/tag/v2.9.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agda v2.9.0</span><span class='zh'>Agda v2.9.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode. The release also includes substantial performance improvements and drops support for GHC 8.8, 8.10, and 9.0. Agda is a dependently typed programming language and interactive theorem prover.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。该版本还带来了显著的性能改进，并停止支持 GHC 8.8、8.10 和 9.0。Agda 是一种依赖类型编程语言和交互式定理证明器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Viktotovich/vb-harness-for-agentic-building" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VB Harness – Make local coding agents verify their own work</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness——让本地编程智能体自行验证工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VB Harness makes local coding agents work in small tasks and isolated copies, using a project’s own checks to validate each change.</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness 将本地编程智能体的工作拆分为小任务，并在隔离副本中执行，通过项目自身的检查来验证每次修改。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VB Harness makes local coding agents work in small tasks and isolated copies, using a project’s own checks to validate each change. It supports LM Studio, Ollama, and other OpenAI-compatible models, and retains changes only when type checks, linting, builds, tests, and independent review succeed. The tool is designed to make local agents accountable without trying to make the underlying model smarter.</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness 将本地编程智能体的工作拆分为小任务，并在隔离副本中执行，通过项目自身的检查来验证每次修改。它支持 LM Studio、Ollama 和其他兼容 OpenAI API 的模型，只有在类型检查、代码检查、构建、测试和独立复审均通过后才会保留改动。该工具旨在让本地智能体对结果负责，而不是试图直接提升底层模型的智能程度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50550829' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50492269" data-id="50492269" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="7" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50492269" data-time="1794617098">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Getterbetter/ember/releases/tag/v1.0.1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Light control for MacBook charger status light (open-source)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：开源控制 MacBook 充电器状态灯</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project lets users control the status light on a MacBook charger.</span><span class='zh'>这个开源项目可以控制 MacBook 充电器的状态灯。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project lets users control the status light on a MacBook charger. It supports turning the light on or off automatically based on time or Focus status, as well as keeping it persistently on or off. The project invites feature suggestions through GitHub.</span><span class='zh'>这个开源项目可以控制 MacBook 充电器的状态灯。它支持根据时间或专注模式状态自动开关灯，也可以让灯持续亮起或关闭。项目通过 GitHub 征集功能建议。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ri4dH/failtrace" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FailTrace – Reproduce a failed GitHub Actions run locally from its URL</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace：通过 URL 在本地复现失败的 GitHub Actions 运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FailTrace takes the URL of a failed GitHub Actions run and identifies the failed job and step.</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace 接收失败的 GitHub Actions 运行 URL，并找出失败的作业和步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FailTrace takes the URL of a failed GitHub Actions run and identifies the failed job and step. It matches that failure to the workflow file at the exact commit, extracts the relevant log output, and explains what is needed to reproduce the issue locally. The tool is designed to reduce guesswork when debugging CI failures.</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace 接收失败的 GitHub Actions 运行 URL，并找出失败的作业和步骤。它会将失败过程与对应提交中的工作流文件匹配，提取相关日志，并说明在本地复现问题所需的条件。该工具旨在减少调试 CI 失败时的猜测。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554941' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50511761" data-id="50511761" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50511761" data-time="1794783217">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/itsmeyaw/skillset" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Skillset: Easily curate and install agent skills collection</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Skillset，轻松整理并安装代理技能集合</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Skillset groups programming, backend, and other agent skills into collections that can be installed with one command from a JSON file.</span><span class='zh'>Skillset 将编程、后端等代理技能整理成集合，用户可以通过一个 JSON 文件和一条命令安装所需集合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Skillset groups programming, backend, and other agent skills into collections that can be installed with one command from a JSON file. It wraps the Vercel Skill CLI and uses it as the underlying library, aiming to preserve compatibility with the standard CLI across teams. The project is designed to avoid installing unnecessary skills globally and consuming excess context.</span><span class='zh'>Skillset 将编程、后端等代理技能整理成集合，用户可以通过一个 JSON 文件和一条命令安装所需集合。它封装并直接使用 Vercel Skill CLI，旨在保持与标准 CLI 的团队间兼容性。该项目希望避免全局安装不必要的技能并消耗过多上下文。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50511761' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article></main><footer class='foot wrap'>Hacker News Daily · <span class='en'>Source</span><span class='zh'>来源</span>: <a href='https://news.ycombinator.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>news.ycombinator.com</a> · <a href='/hackernews/feed.xml'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M4 11a9 9 0 0 1 9 9M4 4a16 16 0 0 1 16 16'/><circle cx='5' cy='19' r='1'/></svg> RSS</a></footer><nav class='tabbar'><a class='on' href='/hackernews/2026/11/20/top_stories_11202026'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M3 10.5 12 3l9 7.5V21h-6v-6H9v6H3z'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Today</span><span class='zh'>今日</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/#archive'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><rect x='3' y='4' width='18' height='18' rx='2'/><path d='M16 2v4M8 2v4M3 10h18'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Archive</span><span class='zh'>往期</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/trends/'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M3 3v18h18'/><path d='m7 15 4-4 3 3 6-7'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Trends</span><span class='zh'>趋势</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/favorites/'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='m12 2 3.1 6.3 6.9 1-5 4.9 1.2 6.8L12 17.8 5.8 21l1.2-6.8-5-4.9 6.9-1z'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Saved</span><span class='zh'>收藏</span></span></a></nav>
